# Stage 05
## Blocks
| Block | Guide section | Learning points / contexts | Cards | ID range |
|---|---|---|---:|---|
| C1a | 6.6 | Precise qualification: degree, probability, limits, approximate scope and exceptions. Academic/ordinary assessments; uncertainty and scope cues visible. P; B2 stance and comparisons. | 100 | ru-05-0001–ru-05-0100 |
| C1b | 6.6, 13 | Evidence strength, attribution, inference versus observation and qualified causal language. Short reports and research discussion; preserve source uncertainty. P; reported speech, B2 source comparison. | 100 | ru-05-0101–ru-05-0200 |
| C1c | 6.6, 9 | Source synthesis: agreement, divergence, complementarity, limits and attributed conclusions in self-contained micro-evidence. P; B2o and C1a–b. Hide relation/collocation; no hidden whole explanation or invented real citation. | 100 | ru-05-0201–ru-05-0300 |
| C1d | 6.6 | Formal argumentation: grounds, concessions, objections, rebuttal, consequence and qualified conclusion. Policy-neutral workplace/academic proposals; explicit relation. P; B2 argument and hypotheses. | 100 | ru-05-0301–ru-05-0400 |
| C1e | 6.6, 12 | Implicit meaning/stance, distancing, presupposition and indirect doubt. Meetings, reviews and messages; a short contrast makes implication interpretable. P; discourse particles, C1 qualification. | 100 | ru-05-0401–ru-05-0500 |
| C1f | 6.6, 11 | Advanced collocations for evidence, explanation, evaluation and methodology. Research/study/professional texts; meaningful domain cue and government visible. P; B2 derivation and formal syntax. | 100 | ru-05-0501–ru-05-0600 |
| C1g | 6.6, 11 | Advanced collocations for responsibility, resources, decisions and consequences. Neutral professional and social contexts; select exact sense without inflating jargon. P; earlier government and B2 negotiation. | 100 | ru-05-0601–ru-05-0700 |
| C1h | 6.6 | Academic nominal style, relation of claims/data/limitations and complex complements. Short original academic propositions; avoid genitive ambiguity. P written; B2i, C1 source functions. | 100 | ru-05-0701–ru-05-0800 |
| C1i | 6.6 | Bureaucratic nominal style, official correspondence, decisions and procedural conditions. Applications, responses, service procedures; distinguish authority/obligation from suggestion. P/R official formula labeled; B2 nominal and request frames. | 100 | ru-05-0801–ru-05-0900 |
| C1j | 6.6, 8 | Written versus conversational syntax, unpacking dense language without changing participant, time or commitment. Messages, explanations and brief editing repairs; source relation recoverable. P; B2 participial/nominal syntax. | 100 | ru-05-0901–ru-05-1000 |
| C1k | 6.6, 12 | Indirect criticism and tactful disagreement: mitigation, concession, face-saving clarification and boundaries. Colleagues, friends and service interactions; social relation/purpose explicit. P; B2n and C1e. | 100 | ru-05-1001–ru-05-1100 |
| C1l | 6.6, 12 | Register switching: peer chat, supervisor/client correspondence and public explanation; exact synonym/formula choice. P standard, context names relationship; B1 ты/вы, B2 register-sensitive vocabulary. | 100 | ru-05-1101–ru-05-1200 |
| C1m | 6.6 | Professional meetings: agenda, turn management, summarizing decisions, assigning work, deadlines and dissent. Actor and commitment visible. P; negotiation, C1 collocations. | 100 | ru-05-1201–ru-05-1300 |
| C1n | 6.6 | Presentations: framing, signposting, qualification, audience questions and accessible explanation. Short presentation excerpts with visible referent. P; source/argument relations, familiar professional topics. | 100 | ru-05-1301–ru-05-1400 |
| C1o | 6.6, 12 | Culturally appropriate address, patronymics/titles/diminutives, distance/solidarity and polite repair. Invitations, colleagues, senior contacts and friends; relation and stance stated naturally in Russian. P/R variants scoped; foundational address and C1 register. | 100 | ru-05-1401–ru-05-1500 |
| C1p | 6.6, 7–8 | Fast multi-speaker comprehension components: response particles, backchannels, ellipsis, repair and reference in two short turns. P/R; actual rate/listening assessment excluded. Preceding turn supplies antecedent; B2 colloquial and C1 meetings. | 100 | ru-05-1501–ru-05-1600 |
| C1q | 6.6, 7, 12 | Accent/generational variation recognition and Standard Russian reformulation; stress/phrase emphasis in precise attitudes. R labeled; standard answer remains Cyrillic, no caricature or unsupported dialect spelling. P concise standard equivalent where target assigned. Prerequisite sound foundation and C1 social stance. | 100 | ru-05-1601–ru-05-1700 |
| C1r | 6.6, 13 | Editing for clarity: ambiguous reference, nominal chains, redundant collocations, scope/negation and misplaced modifiers. Hide repaired word/compact construction with visible contrast; preserve actor, uncertainty and commitment. P; prior C1 systems. Also neutral everyday/social contexts to balance formal registers. | 100 | ru-05-1701–ru-05-1800 |
## Cards
| ID | Block | Hidden learning point |
|---|---|---|
| ru-05-0001 | C1a | other things being equal; При governs the prepositional plural forms прочих равных; условиях is omitted in this fixed expression. The comparison of prices assumes that the other relevant conditions are equal. |
| ru-05-0002 | C1a | to what extent; Насколько introduces an indirect question about the degree of dependence. |
| ru-05-0003 | C1a | partly; Отчасти limits the assessment of the conclusion; the following clause identifies a limitation. |
| ru-05-0004 | C1a | substantially; Существенно qualifies the degree of difference expressed by ниже. |
| ru-05-0005 | C1a | so; Настолько qualifies мала; the что-clause states the consequence of that degree. |
| ru-05-0006 | C1a | in all likelihood; По governs dative feminine singular вероятности, with agreeing всей. The expression presents delivery as highly likely but still unconfirmed. |
| ru-05-0007 | C1a | with high probability; С governs instrumental feminine singular вероятностью, with agreeing большой. The absence of the keys from the bag supports a likely inference, but does not establish where they are. |
| ru-05-0008 | C1a | possibly; The parenthetical word presents the cause as a possibility; the source file cannot be checked. |
| ru-05-0009 | C1a | is small; Short feminine singular adjective agreeing with вероятность. Невелика assesses the probability as low without saying that rain is impossible. |
| ru-05-0010 | C1a | most likely; Вероятен is a short masculine singular adjective agreeing with снег; наиболее marks the highest degree. The forecast identifies the night as the time when snow is most likely. |
| ru-05-0011 | C1a | approximately; The adverb marks 30 as an approximate count. |
| ru-05-0012 | C1a | approximately; Порядка governs the genitive quantity трёх недель and marks it as approximate. |
| ru-05-0013 | C1a | about forty people; Человек is genitive plural; reversing the usual numeral–noun order marks an approximate count. |
| ru-05-0014 | C1a | to a precision of; С governs instrumental feminine singular точностью; до governs genitive миллиметра. The expression states the precision of the reported measurement. |
| ru-05-0015 | C1a | within the margin of error; В governs prepositional plural пределах; пределах is followed by genitive feminine singular погрешности. The one-gram difference is smaller than the stated two-gram error bound. |
| ru-05-0016 | C1a | strictly speaking; В governs prepositional masculine singular смысле, with agreeing строгом. The phrase limits the classification to the strict sense of translation. |
| ru-05-0017 | C1a | adjusted for; С governs instrumental feminine singular поправкой; на governs accusative размер. The comparison adjusts attendance for the size of the groups. |
| ru-05-0018 | C1a | at least; По governs dative singular мере, with agreeing меньшей, in this fixed phrase. The phrase sets one hour as a lower bound for the estimated duration. |
| ru-05-0019 | C1a | no more than; Не свыше sets an inclusive upper limit and governs the genitive quantity. |
| ru-05-0020 | C1a | no less than; Не менее sets an inclusive lower bound and governs genitive двух лет. |
| ru-05-0021 | C1a | in addition to; Помимо governs genitive словаря and adds the grammar reference to the permitted materials. |
| ru-05-0022 | C1a | after deducting; За governs instrumental masculine singular вычетом in this compound preposition. За вычетом governs genitive сотни and introduces the amount deducted from the total. |
| ru-05-0023 | C1a | except for; Кроме governs genitive договора and excludes the contract from the documents that may be emailed. |
| ru-05-0024 | C1a | including; Включая introduces a group explicitly included within всех сотрудников. |
| ru-05-0025 | C1a | irrespective of; Безотносительно к governs dative известности and excludes the author's prominence from the assessment. |
| ru-05-0026 | C1a | excluding; Без governs genitive singular учёта in this prepositional phrase. Без учёта governs genitive доставки and states that delivery is excluded from the quoted price. |
| ru-05-0027 | C1a | as applied to; Применительно к governs dative детям and limits the claim about the rule to the specified group. |
| ru-05-0028 | C1a | with rare exceptions; За governs instrumental neuter singular исключением, with agreeing редким. The phrase states that almost all participants read the article, allowing for rare exceptions. |
| ru-05-0029 | C1a | predominantly; The adverb qualifies the overall distribution of opinions, allowing for exceptions. |
| ru-05-0030 | C1a | as a rule; The expression states a usual pattern rather than an exceptionless rule; summer is explicitly contrasted. |
| ru-05-0031 | C1a | usually; Обычно marks the normal arrangement; the snow condition introduces an exception. |
| ru-05-0032 | C1a | in individual cases; В governs prepositional plural случаях, with agreeing отдельных. The phrase restricts late acceptance to individual cases; illness is given as an example. |
| ru-05-0033 | C1a | at least; По governs dative singular мере, with agreeing крайней, in this fixed phrase. The phrase sets a lower bound of one participant and leaves the actual number open. |
| ru-05-0034 | C1a | only insofar as; The construction limits the reliability of the conclusion to the degree of accuracy of the source data. |
| ru-05-0035 | C1a | as a first approximation; В governs prepositional neuter singular приближении, with agreeing первом. The flat-wall model is an initial approximation that will be refined. |
| ru-05-0036 | C1a | approximate; Short feminine singular adjective agreeing with оценка. The estimate lacks precision because the exact dimensions of the plot have not yet been measured. |
| ru-05-0037 | C1a | at best; В governs prepositional singular случае, with agreeing лучшем. The phrase presents tomorrow as the best expected arrival time under the stated condition. |
| ru-05-0038 | C1a | at worst; В governs prepositional singular случае, with agreeing худшем. The phrase presents replacement of the whole screen as the worst contemplated outcome. |
| ru-05-0039 | C1a | as far as one can judge; The impersonal modal можно combines with the infinitive судить. Судить is imperfective and belongs to the second conjugation; a present form is судит. This epistemic use has no simple perfective partner. Судить по governs the dative evidence; the infinitive has no tense of its own and here qualifies a present assessment. The construction limits the inference to what can be judged from one photograph. |
| ru-05-0040 | C1a | apparently; По governs dative singular видимости, with agreeing всей, in this fixed phrase. The phrase marks an inference; the traffic jam supplies its visible basis. |
| ru-05-0041 | C1a | without exaggeration; Без governs genitive singular neuter преувеличения. Assures the listener that the count is literal, supported by a count of everyone present. |
| ru-05-0042 | C1a | presumably; Marks the key's suitability as an assumption awaiting a check. |
| ru-05-0043 | C1a | justifiably; Насколько asks how justified the assessment is, given that only volunteers responded. |
| ru-05-0044 | C1a | in principle; В governs prepositional singular masculine принципе. Marks construction as feasible in principle, despite the current lack of funding. |
| ru-05-0045 | C1a | evidently; Marks the speaker's inference from the open window and light. |
| ru-05-0046 | C1a | perhaps; Softens the speaker's decision to take an umbrella. |
| ru-05-0047 | C1a | it looks as though; Introduces a tentative inference from the stopped, unresponsive clock. |
| ru-05-0048 | C1a | as if; Presents the knocking as an uncertain impression, qualified by the empty landing. |
| ru-05-0049 | C1a | it is unlikely; Expresses doubt that the work can be finished today; the missing parts give the reason. |
| ru-05-0050 | C1a | is due to arrive; Short masculine singular должен agrees with поезд; прибыть is an infinitive. Прибыть is perfective; imperfective прибывать. Future forms include прибудет and прибудут. It describes arrival at a destination; the infinitive here is governed by должен and refers to a scheduled future event. Прибыть denotes reaching a destination; the prefixed perfective describes a completed arrival rather than a base directionality contrast. The verb itself does not specify the means of travel. Должен expresses an expectation based on the timetable, not an obligation imposed on the train. |
| ru-05-0051 | C1a | arguably the best; Лучший is nominative singular masculine, referring to план. Едва ли не qualifies the superlative ranking among the proposed plans; пожалуй and the unresolved details reinforce the reservation. |
| ru-05-0052 | C1a | if necessary; При governs prepositional singular необходимости. Makes an extension conditional on its being needed. |
| ru-05-0053 | C1a | in the absence of; При governs prepositional singular отсутствии; подписи is dependent genitive. Introduces the absence of a signature as the condition for returning the application. |
| ru-05-0054 | C1a | if possible; По governs dative singular возможности in this fixed phrase. Softens the request by making today's submission dependent on feasibility. |
| ru-05-0055 | C1a | in case of; На governs accusative singular случай; дождя is dependent genitive. Introduces the possible event for which the hall was reserved. |
| ru-05-0056 | C1a | even in; При governs the prepositional phrase сильном ветре. Even under the adverse condition of strong wind, the stated stability holds. |
| ru-05-0057 | C1a | for want of a better option; За governs instrumental singular неимением; substantivized лучшего is genitive singular. Explains the choice of the hall as a fallback because the other options were unavailable. |
| ru-05-0058 | C1a | until; До governs the genitive phrase проверки оборудования. Sets the equipment check as the endpoint of the provisional closure. |
| ru-05-0059 | C1a | at present; На governs accusative singular момент with agreeing данный. Limits the statement about available places to the present time. |
| ru-05-0060 | C1a | a working hypothesis; Genitive singular feminine after в качестве; рабочей agrees with версии. В качестве introduces the sensor error as a provisional hypothesis to be checked. |
| ru-05-0061 | C1a | with some qualification; С governs instrumental singular feminine долей with agreeing известной; условности is genitive singular. Qualifies путеводитель as an approximate label for the one-page memo. |
| ru-05-0062 | C1a | in broad outline; В governs prepositional plural чертах with agreeing общих. Limits understanding to the broad outline; the missing dates illustrate a detail. |
| ru-05-0063 | C1a | in substance; По governs dative singular существу. Limits the positive assessment to the substance of the remark, contrasted with its wording. |
| ru-05-0064 | C1a | in formal terms; Limits compliance to the report's required format, contrasting it with unchecked calculations. |
| ru-05-0065 | C1a | in theory; Limits the possibility to theory; unavailable parts present a practical obstacle. |
| ru-05-0066 | C1a | virtually all; Все is nominative plural, agreeing with лампы. Practically all lamps went out; the light at the exit is the visible exception. |
| ru-05-0067 | C1a | roughly, at a guess; Describes an informal estimate made without measuring; the missing tape measure gives the context. |
| ru-05-0068 | C1a | by a rough estimate; По governs dative singular оценке with agreeing грубой. Marks the prediction about paint coverage as an imprecise estimate. |
| ru-05-0069 | C1a | by eye; На governs accusative singular глаз in this fixed expression. Marks the comparison as a visual estimate; a further check is planned. |
| ru-05-0070 | C1a | within the range from; В governs prepositional singular диапазоне; от introduces the lower endpoint in the genitive. Pairs with до to specify the range within which the temperature fluctuated. |
| ru-05-0071 | C1a | in places; Limits the stated wind speed to some locations, as reinforced by не повсюду. |
| ru-05-0072 | C1a | at times; Limits the below-zero temperature to intermittent periods during the day. |
| ru-05-0073 | C1a | without a single delay; Без governs genitive singular опоздания with agreeing единого. Emphasizes the complete absence of delays. |
| ru-05-0074 | C1a | without additional expenses; Без governs genitive plural расходов with agreeing дополнительных. Qualifies the early completion by specifying that the client incurred no extra expenses. |
| ru-05-0075 | C1a | by the standards of; По governs dative plural меркам; города is dependent genitive. Sets the standard against which the museum's size is assessed. |
| ru-05-0076 | C1a | taking into account; С governs instrumental singular учётом; доставки is dependent genitive. Includes delivery as a factor in the assessment of the price. |
| ru-05-0077 | C1a | in respect of; В governs prepositional singular части; оплаты is dependent genitive. Limits the contract change to payment, while the other terms remain intact. |
| ru-05-0078 | C1a | with regard to; В governs prepositional singular отношении; сроков is dependent genitive. Limits the assessment of clarity to deadlines, contrasting them with payment terms. |
| ru-05-0079 | C1a | only in one respect; В governs prepositional singular отношении with agreeing одном; лишь restricts the scope. Limits the stated resemblance to one respect, identified after the colon. |
| ru-05-0080 | C1a | strictly within; В governs prepositional plural пределах; отдела is dependent genitive. Restricts the scope of the arrangement to the speaker's department. |
| ru-05-0081 | C1a | financially; Prepositional singular плане with agreeing финансовом after в. Limits the positive assessment to finances; visitor numbers provide a contrasting measure. |
| ru-05-0082 | C1a | to the best of one's ability; В governs accusative singular меру; сил is genitive plural. Limits the help to what the speaker can manage; the available hour is an additional visible constraint. |
| ru-05-0083 | C1a | conventionally; Marks the division as a convenient convention rather than a sharp natural boundary. |
| ru-05-0084 | C1a | depending on; The compound preposition governs genitive сезона. Introduces the factor according to which prices vary. |
| ru-05-0085 | C1a | with one exception; За governs instrumental singular исключением with agreeing одним. Introduces the single exception specified after the colon. |
| ru-05-0086 | C1a | to a large extent; В governs the prepositional form многом. Qualifies the degree of dependence without claiming it is complete. |
| ru-05-0087 | C1a | far from everyone; Всем is dative plural, governed by подходит; далеко не strengthens the limitation. Denies that the method suits a substantial part of the potential users. |
| ru-05-0088 | C1a | not so much; Pairs with сколько to rank sales as less important than the stated alternative. |
| ru-05-0089 | C1a | not least; В governs accusative singular feminine очередь, with agreeing последнюю. Gives delivery significant causal weight alongside the contribution of advertising. |
| ru-05-0090 | C1a | by a factor of ten; На governs accusative singular masculine порядок. Expresses an exact tenfold decrease in this numerical context. |
| ru-05-0091 | C1a | limited; Short masculine singular adjective agreeing with срок. Describes the duration as finite; the following clause specifies its endpoint. |
| ru-05-0092 | C1a | except for; The infinitive считать governs accusative singular доставку. Считать is imperfective and first conjugation; compare считает. This fixed exclusion formula has no productive perfective partner. Excludes delivery from the stated cost rather than setting a literal counting condition. |
| ru-05-0093 | C1a | by mutual agreement; По governs dative singular согласию with agreeing взаимному. States that both parties must agree to the extension. |
| ru-05-0094 | C1a | irrespective of; Вне governs genitive singular зависимости; от governs genitive возраста. States that the free service applies regardless of the visitor's age. |
| ru-05-0095 | C1a | approximately the same; Instrumental masculine singular одинаковым describes результат after получился. Approximates equality between the results rather than an individual quantity. |
| ru-05-0096 | C1a | most likely; Presents a May opening as likely, based on the progress of the renovation, without treating it as certain. |
| ru-05-0097 | C1a | at a stretch; С governs instrumental singular feminine натяжкой. Marks the classification as a compromise as strained because only one side is satisfied. |
| ru-05-0098 | C1a | right up to; К governs dative singular лимиту. With подойти, describes approaching a limit very closely; the negation denies that stage has been reached. |
| ru-05-0099 | C1a | from the standpoint of; The compound preposition governs genitive безопасности. Names the criterion of assessment, contrasted with cost. |
| ru-05-0100 | C1a | not everyone; Все is nominative plural, agreeing with участники; не negates universal scope. States that agreement was not universal, without saying nobody agreed. |
| ru-05-0101 | C1b | indirectly; Qualifies the indication as indirect; falling pressure is the observed clue. |
| ru-05-0102 | C1b | are visible; Short plural adjective agreeing with трещины. Distinguishes what can be observed in the image from the unknown depth. |
| ru-05-0103 | C1b | needs to be checked; Third-person singular present reflexive нуждается agrees with утверждение; в governs prepositional singular проверке. Нуждаться is imperfective and first conjugation; compare нуждаются. It has no simple perfective partner for this state. Нуждаться в чём takes the prepositional; the present describes a current need. Calls for independent checking without asserting that the claim is false. |
| ru-05-0104 | C1b | primary data; Nominative plural; первичные agrees with данные, the subject of содержат. Names the original evidence rather than a statistical summary or interpretation. |
| ru-05-0105 | C1b | presumed; Nominative singular feminine adjective agreeing with причина. Marks the proposed cause as unconfirmed; inspection is still pending. |
| ru-05-0106 | C1b | only one; Одного is genitive singular masculine, agreeing with датчика. Restricts the source of the reported readings to one sensor. |
| ru-05-0107 | C1b | suggests itself; Third-person singular present reflexive, agreeing with вывод. Напрашиваться is imperfective and first conjugation; compare напрашиваются. It has no simple perfective partner in this figurative inference sense. The present describes a conclusion that suggests itself; о governs the prepositional topic. The repeated shutdowns suggest overheating as their cause, but that causal explanation has not been verified. |
| ru-05-0108 | C1b | does not necessarily mean; Third-person singular present означает agrees with связь. Означать is imperfective, with no simple perfective partner in this relational sense. First conjugation; compare означаю and означают. A что-clause states the proposed implication; the present describes the logical relation. Denies that causation necessarily follows from the observed association. |
| ru-05-0109 | C1b | reproducibility; Accusative singular feminine, the direct object of подтвердив; результата is dependent genitive. Refers to obtaining the same outcome under the same conditions in another group's repetition. |
| ru-05-0110 | C1b | sampling bias; Genitive singular neuter смещения depends on риском; выборки is dependent genitive singular. Names a possible bias when answers from runners alone are generalized to all town residents. |
| ru-05-0111 | C1b | a consequence; Instrumental singular neuter after назван; рекламы is dependent genitive. Attributes the causal interpretation to the report; other causes were not studied. |
| ru-05-0112 | C1b | according to the claim of; По governs dative singular утверждению; компании identifies the source in the genitive. Attributes the battery-life claim to the company without establishing it independently. |
| ru-05-0113 | C1b | discrepancy between estimates; Nominative singular neuter расхождение is the subject of требует; оценок is dependent genitive plural. Names the difference between the two estimates without deciding which is correct. |
| ru-05-0114 | C1b | on the basis of; На governs prepositional singular основании; интервью is the dependent genitive source. Introduces the evidence base judged inadequate for a conclusion about the whole department. |
| ru-05-0115 | C1b | causal connection; Nominative singular feminine; причинная agrees with связь, the subject of не установлена. Distinguishes a causal relationship from the stated coincidence in time. |
| ru-05-0116 | C1b | automatically; With нельзя распространить, cautions against extending the findings to everyone without further justification. |
| ru-05-0117 | C1b | independently of each other; От governs the genitive form друга in the reciprocal expression. States that the witnesses gave their accounts independently. |
| ru-05-0118 | C1b | remains unknown; Third-person singular present остаётся agrees with дата; неизвестной is instrumental feminine singular. Оставаться is imperfective; perfective остаться. First conjugation; compare остаюсь and остаются. It takes an instrumental description here; the present states a continuing condition. Marks the letter's date as still unknown despite its quotation. |
| ru-05-0119 | C1b | are consistent with; Third-person plural present reflexive agrees with результаты; с governs instrumental гипотезой. Согласоваться is biaspectual; here it has an imperfective compatibility reading and no distinct perfective partner for the continuing relation. First conjugation; compare согласу́ется. С takes the instrumental; the present describes consistency with a hypothesis. Compatibility does not establish the hypothesis as the only explanation. |
| ru-05-0120 | C1b | each factor's possible contribution; Nominative singular masculine вклад, with agreeing возможный, is the subject of не оценён; каждого фактора is dependent genitive singular. Leaves each factor's contribution unestimated and its causal role possible rather than established. |
| ru-05-0121 | C1b | recorded; Masculine singular past, agreeing with датчик. Зафиксировать is biaspectual; here it presents a completed recording. Compare зафиксирует. It takes an accusative object. The verb reports a recorded measurement without identifying its cause. |
| ru-05-0122 | C1b | reported; Plural past, agreeing with the quantified subject «Пять из двадцати участников»; о governs prepositional «боли». Сообщить is perfective; imperfective сообщать. Compare сообщат. Сообщить о чём names the subject of a completed report. The participants report their own experience; the sentence does not establish what caused it. |
| ru-05-0123 | C1b | is audible; Short masculine singular adjective, agreeing with хлопок. The recording supports an observation about sound, while the source of the sound is not visible. |
| ru-05-0124 | C1b | observations; Genitive plural neuter, dependent on журнале. Журнал наблюдений names a record of observations; the description does not establish the spots' composition. |
| ru-05-0125 | C1b | a trend; Prepositional singular feminine after о. Тенденция к снижению describes a direction of change without implying a steady decrease at every point. |
| ru-05-0126 | C1b | does not constitute reliable evidence; Negative third-person singular present служит agrees with отзыв; надёжным свидетельством is instrumental singular neuter. Служить is imperfective, with no simple perfective partner in this relational sense. Compare служат. Служить чем takes the instrumental; the present states an evidential relation. The phrase assesses whether the single review is reliable evidence for the quality of the whole batch. |
| ru-05-0127 | C1b | lower bound; Nominative singular feminine; нижняя agrees with граница, used as the predicate. The recorded amount is a lower bound on total expenditure because only some expenses have been included. |
| ru-05-0128 | C1b | only; Лишь restricts the survey's contribution to an approximate picture. |
| ru-05-0129 | C1b | based on; The complex preposition governs genitive этих измерений. Introduces the evidence used for the qualified inference expressed by могла. |
| ru-05-0130 | C1b | probably; The parenthetical marks the diagnosis as probable, based on the contrasting readings. |

| ru-05-0457 | C1e | is vague; Short feminine singular adjective agreeing with фраза. Characterizes the wording as insufficiently precise; the missing period and amount explain the assessment. |

The reply may encourage the listener, but it does not establish agreement. |

| ru-05-0476 | C1e | I cannot venture to judge; Negated first-person singular present reflexive берусь is followed by the infinitive судить. | ru-05-0553 | C1f | long-term consequences; Accusative plural; долгосрочные agrees with последствия, an object of учитывала. The phrase names consequences considered over a long period, alongside costs. |
| ru-05-0554 | C1f | arithmetic mean; Accusative singular neuter; the substantive phrase is the object of вычислили. Трёх измерений identifies the values whose arithmetic mean was calculated. |
| ru-05-0555 | C1f | labels; Instrumental plural; условными agrees with обозначениями. Заменить что чем takes the instrumental for what replaces the original; here labels replace names. |
| ru-05-0556 | C1f | actual sales; Instrumental plural after с; фактическими agrees with продажами. Сопоставить что с чем uses с and the instrumental for the comparison; actual sales are compared with the forecast. |
| ru-05-0557 | C1f | as percentages; В governs prepositional plural процентах. The phrase specifies the form of presentation; от общего числа identifies the denominator. |
| ru-05-0558 | C1f | definition of a key concept; Accusative singular neuter head with a dependent genitive singular phrase; object of уточнили. The phrase identifies the definition revised in response to the reviewer's comment. |
| ru-05-0559 | C1f | broken down by age; С governs instrumental singular разбивкой; по governs dative singular возрасту. The phrase identifies age as one dimension used to divide the results into groups. |
| ru-05-0560 | C1f | assessment of effectiveness; Accusative singular feminine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of провести. The phrase names an assessment of how effective something is; программы specifies what is assessed. |
| ru-05-0561 | C1f | systematic error; Nominative singular feminine; the adjective agrees with погрешность, the subject of смещала. The phrase names a consistent component of measurement error; the upward shift illustrates its direction. |
| ru-05-0562 | C1f | numerator; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; one of the objects of определили. Числитель is the upper part of a fraction, contrasted here with знаменатель. |
| ru-05-0563 | C1f | independent variable; Nominative singular feminine; независимая agrees with переменная, the subject of обозначает. The term names a variable used to explain or predict an outcome in a model. |
| ru-05-0564 | C1f | null hypothesis; Nominative singular feminine; нулевая agrees with гипотеза, the subject of указана. The clause after the colon states the null hypothesis being tested. |
| ru-05-0565 | C1f | study protocol; Accusative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of составила. The phrase names a document specifying how the study is to be conducted. |
| ru-05-0566 | C1f | interviewer instructions; Accusative singular feminine head, object of подготовила, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase names instructions for the person conducting an interview; the sentence specifies rules for the conversation. |
| ru-05-0567 | C1f | randomization procedure; Nominative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; subject of описан. Рандомизация names allocation by a random procedure; участников identifies the people allocated. |
| ru-05-0568 | C1f | sensitivity threshold; Genitive singular masculine head after выше, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase names the threshold above which the instrument can detect the change. |
| ru-05-0569 | C1f | range of values; Nominative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive plural noun; subject of приведён. The phrase identifies the span of values; от and до mark its endpoints. |
| ru-05-0570 | C1f | main analysis; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after в; основной agrees with анализ. The phrase distinguishes the principal analysis from the separate examination of excluded cases. |
| ru-05-0571 | C1f | spread of readings; Accusative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive plural noun; object of оценили. Разброс describes how widely the readings vary. |
| ru-05-0572 | C1f | questionnaire text; Accusative singular masculine head, object of приложить, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase names the questionnaire's wording as a document available for inspection. |
| ru-05-0573 | C1f | coded; Past plural, agreeing with два специалиста. Кодировать is imperfective here; закодировать can describe a completed act. First conjugation; compare кодируют. It takes an accusative object; the past describes the coding process. In research, кодировать ответы means assigning categories or codes to responses. |
| ru-05-0574 | C1f | agreement between codes; Accusative singular neuter head with a dependent genitive plural noun; object of проверили. The phrase concerns whether the codes assigned by the two specialists match. |
| ru-05-0575 | C1f | effect of scale; Accusative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of учли. In this economic context, the phrase concerns how production scale affects costs. |
| ru-05-0576 | C1f | generalizability; Nominative singular feminine; subject of ограничена. Обобщаемость concerns the extent to which a finding can be applied beyond the cases studied. |
| ru-05-0577 | C1f | sample composition; Accusative singular masculine head after на, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase concerns which kinds of participants make up the sample. |
| ru-05-0578 | C1f | informed consent; Accusative singular neuter; информированное agrees with согласие, the object of получили. The phrase names consent given after relevant information has been provided. |
| ru-05-0579 | C1f | de-identification of records; Genitive singular neuter head dependent on порядок, with a dependent genitive plural noun. The phrase names the removal or alteration of identifying information in records. |
| ru-05-0580 | C1f | mean error; Accusative singular feminine; среднюю agrees with ошибку, the object of вычислили. The phrase names an average of prediction errors; the precise calculation requires further specification. |
| ru-05-0581 | C1f | trained; Past plural with an unspecified human subject. Imperfective обучать; perfective обучить. First conjugation; compare обучают. It takes an accusative object; the past describes the training process. Обучать модель is the technical use for fitting a model using data. |
| ru-05-0582 | C1f | test set; Accusative singular feminine; тестовую agrees with выборку after как. The phrase names the data set used to assess the forecast. |
| ru-05-0583 | C1f | dataset; Accusative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive plural noun; object of использовали. Набор данных names a collection of data treated as a unit for analysis. |
| ru-05-0584 | C1f | exclusion criteria; Nominative plural masculine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; subject of описаны. The phrase names conditions for excluding potential participants; the next clause gives one. |
| ru-05-0585 | C1f | data cutoff date; Accusative singular feminine head with dependent genitives; object of указали. The date marks the cutoff for records included in the analysis. |
| ru-05-0586 | C1f | extreme values; Genitive plural; крайних agrees with значений, which depends on удаления. The phrase identifies values at the extremes of the observed range. |
| ru-05-0587 | C1f | point system; Dative singular feminine head after по, with a dependent genitive plural noun. По системе баллов identifies the scoring framework used to evaluate the projects. |
| ru-05-0588 | C1f | optimistic and pessimistic; Masculine singular adjectives referring to сценарий, understood after each adjective. The adjectives contrast scenarios with more favorable and less favorable assumptions. |
| ru-05-0589 | C1f | sources of error; Accusative plural masculine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of указать. The phrase names factors that can introduce error into a measurement. |
| ru-05-0590 | C1f | indicator; Nominative singular masculine; subject of подходит. Here показатель is a measure used to assess a specified property. |
| ru-05-0591 | C1f | baseline values; Nominative plural; исходные agrees with значения, the subject of приведены. Исходные значения are starting values used as a reference for later comparison. |
| ru-05-0592 | C1f | coding scheme; Accusative singular feminine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of разработала. The phrase names the system used to assign codes to the responses. |
| ru-05-0593 | C1f | field notes; Instrumental plural after с; полевыми agrees with заметками. Полевые заметки are notes made during fieldwork; с identifies the material used for comparison. |
| ru-05-0594 | C1f | value judgments; Accusative plural; оценочные agrees with суждения, the object of отделили. The phrase names judgments that evaluate something, distinguished here from a description of results. |
| ru-05-0595 | C1f | preliminary data; Accusative plural; предварительные agrees with данные, the object of содержит. The phrase identifies data presented before the final figures are available. |
| ru-05-0596 | C1f | conflict of interest; Nominative singular masculine head with a dependent genitive plural noun; subject of раскрыт. The phrase names a situation in which interests may affect professional judgment; the funding relationship is disclosed. |
| ru-05-0597 | C1f | model sensitivity; Accusative singular feminine head with a dependent genitive singular noun; object of проверили. Чувствительность к takes the dative and concerns how the model responds to a change. |
| ru-05-0598 | C1f | calculation formula; Nominative singular feminine head, subject of приведена, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase names the mathematical expression used for the calculation; доли расходов specifies the quantity calculated. |
| ru-05-0599 | C1f | observation period; Accusative singular masculine head after за, with a dependent genitive singular noun. The phrase names the span of time during which observations are made. |
| ru-05-0600 | C1f | directions for further work; Accusative plural neuter head with a dependent genitive singular phrase; object of обозначили. The phrase identifies possible lines of future work; над моделью specifies its subject. |
| ru-05-0601 | C1g | bears responsibility; Third-person singular present verb with accusative singular ответственность. Нести is imperfective; this responsibility sense has no simple perfective partner. Present forms include несу and несут. Нести ответственность за takes the accusative and describes continuing responsibility. За сохранность документов names what the archive manager is responsible for. |
| ru-05-0602 | C1g | is responsible for; Third-person singular present; за governs accusative качество. Отвечать is imperfective; in this continuing responsibility sense it has no simple perfective partner. Present forms include отвечаю and отвечают. Отвечать за takes the accusative. The contract establishes the contractor's responsibility for the quality of the work. |
| ru-05-0603 | C1g | accountable; Short masculine singular adjective, agreeing grammatically with директор; the person or body to whom the director is accountable is in the dative. Подотчётен совету describes an obligation to account to the council for the use of funds. |
| ru-05-0604 | C1g | delegated; Feminine singular past, agreeing with руководительница. Делегировать is used perfectively here and can also be imperfective. Finite forms include делегирую and делегирует. It takes an accusative object and a dative recipient; the past reports a completed delegation. Часть полномочий names the authority transferred; заместителю identifies its recipient. |
| ru-05-0605 | C1g | authority; Genitive plural neuter inanimate after нет. Полномочия with an infinitive names the authority to perform the specified act. |
| ru-05-0606 | C1g | report on one's actions; Imperfective reflexive infinitive after обязан. Отчитываться is imperfective; perfective отчитаться. Present forms include отчитываюсь and отчитывается. Перед with the instrumental names the body receiving the account; о with the prepositional names its subject. The infinitive expresses an ongoing obligation. The department must give the council an account of how funds are spent. |
| ru-05-0607 | C1g | are delineated; Short past passive participle, plural, agreeing with обязанности. Разграничить is perfective; imperfective разграничивать. Future forms include разграничу and разграничит. It takes an accusative object; the short passive presents the duties as delineated. The statute distinguishes the chair's duties from those of the commission members. |
| ru-05-0608 | C1g | qualification; Genitive singular feminine inanimate after без. Квалификация refers to the competence required for the safety check. |
| ru-05-0609 | C1g | remit; Genitive singular feminine inanimate, dependent on рамки. Выходить за рамки компетенции means to fall outside a person's or body's remit. |
| ru-05-0610 | C1g | disciplinary liability; Nominative singular feminine; дисциплинарная agrees with ответственность, the subject of предусмотрена. За with the accusative names the violation for which disciplinary liability is provided. |
| ru-05-0611 | C1g | has at its disposal; Third-person singular present, agreeing with команда. Располага́ть is imperfective; in this possession sense it has no simple perfective partner. Present forms include располагаю and располагают. Располагать чем takes the instrumental and describes resources currently available. Средствами names the funds at the team's disposal. |
| ru-05-0612 | C1g | reserve; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; object of сохранили. Резерв материалов is a stock kept available for a possible need. |
| ru-05-0613 | C1g | were depleted; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with запасы. Истощиться is perfective; imperfective истощаться. Future forms include истощится and истощатся. The resource is the subject; the past reports its depletion. Запасы бумаги names the stock that ran out. |
| ru-05-0614 | C1g | directed; Masculine singular past, agreeing with фонд. Направить is perfective; imperfective направлять. Future forms include направлю and направит. Направить средства на takes an accusative purpose; the past reports a completed allocation. The funds are directed toward the stated repair. |
| ru-05-0615 | C1g | is underfunded; Short masculine singular past passive participle, agreeing with проект. Недофинансировать can be perfective or imperfective; the short passive describes the resulting state here. Finite forms include недофинансирует and недофинансируют. The verb takes an accusative project or activity. The following clause shows that the allocated sum is insufficient. |
| ru-05-0616 | C1g | ongoing expenses; Accusative plural after на; текущие agrees with расходы. Текущие расходы are costs arising in regular operation. |
| ru-05-0617 | C1g | cost estimate; Prepositional singular feminine inanimate after в. Смета is the planned estimate of costs against which the purchase is checked. |
| ru-05-0618 | C1g | consumption; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; object of сократила. Расход воды is the amount of water used, rather than a monetary expense. |
| ru-05-0619 | C1g | actual costs; Prepositional plural after о; фактических agrees with затратах. Фактические затраты are costs actually incurred, as distinct from estimates. |
| ru-05-0620 | C1g | invested; Past plural, agreeing with добровольцы. Вложить is perfective; imperfective вкладывать. Future forms include вложу and вложит. Вложить что во что takes an accusative resource and в with the accusative for the undertaking; the past reports the effort invested. The volunteers invested time in creating the archive. |
| ru-05-0621 | C1g | reached consensus; Past plural verb, agreeing with участники; консенсуса is genitive singular masculine. Достичь is perfective; imperfective достигать. Future forms include достигну and достигнет. Достичь чего takes the genitive; the past reports a completed result. Достичь консенсуса means to reach general agreement; по срокам identifies the issue agreed upon. |
| ru-05-0622 | C1g | limit; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; object of исчерпал. Лимит расходов is the permitted maximum expenditure; исчерпал indicates that the full allowance has been used. |
| ru-05-0623 | C1g | criterion; Instrumental singular masculine inanimate, predicative after быть. Критерий is a standard used to judge the choice. |
| ru-05-0624 | C1g | initial; Accusative singular neuter, agreeing with решение. Первоначальное identifies the decision made before the later change. |
| ru-05-0625 | C1g | unanimously; Единогласно describes approval by all voting members, with no opposing vote. |
| ru-05-0626 | C1g | abstained; Masculine singular past reflexive; feminine воздержалась is also possible because член совета does not establish gender. Воздержаться is perfective; imperfective воздерживаться. Future forms include воздержусь and воздержится. При голосовании identifies the vote; the past reports a single abstention. Here воздержаться means to abstain from voting for or against. |
| ru-05-0627 | C1g | quorum; Genitive singular masculine inanimate after не было. Кворум is the minimum attendance required for the meeting to make valid decisions. |
| ru-05-0628 | C1g | give up; Perfective reflexive infinitive after пришлось; от governs genitive замысла. Отказаться is perfective; imperfective отказываться. Future forms include откажусь and откажется. Отказаться от takes the genitive; пришлось presents the abandonment as necessary. The group gives up its original plan because of new restrictions. |
| ru-05-0629 | C1g | at the initiative of; По governs dative singular инициативе; участников is a dependent genitive plural. The phrase attributes the proposal to move the meeting to its participants. |
| ru-05-0630 | C1g | essential terms; Accusative plural; существенные agrees with условия, the object of объяснила. The phrase identifies terms considered essential to the contract. |
| ru-05-0631 | C1g | a side effect; Instrumental singular masculine after стало; побочным agrees with эффектом. The phrase identifies an additional effect of the repair, rather than its intended result. |
| ru-05-0632 | C1g | has an effect on; Third-person singular present reflexive, agreeing with нехватка; на governs prepositional внимании. Сказываться is imperfective; perfective сказаться. Present forms include сказываюсь and сказывается. Сказываться на takes the prepositional; the present describes an effect developing over time. Постепенно describes how the effect on the driver's attention emerges. |
| ru-05-0633 | C1g | affected; Neuter singular past, agreeing with изменение. Затронуть is perfective; imperfective затрагивать. Future forms include затрону and затронет. It takes an accusative person or group; the past reports the effect of the change. This sense means to affect people, rather than to bring up a topic. |
| ru-05-0634 | C1g | reversible; Short plural adjective, agreeing with изменения; ли introduces an indirect question. Обратимые изменения are changes whose effects can be reversed. |
| ru-05-0635 | C1g | damage; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; object of снизить. Ущерб от наводнения names the harm caused by the flood. |
| ru-05-0636 | C1g | failure to meet deadlines; Genitive singular masculine head after избежать, with dependent genitive plural сроков. Срыв сроков means a failure to meet agreed or planned deadlines. |
| ru-05-0637 | C1g | lost; Past plural reflexive, agreeing with несколько клиентов. Лишиться is perfective; imperfective лишаться. Future forms include лишусь and лишатся. Лишиться чего takes the genitive; the past reports a loss. Доступа names what the clients lost; к своим документам specifies the access. |
| ru-05-0638 | C1g | will take effect; Third-person plural perfective future; в governs accusative singular силу. Вступить is perfective; imperfective вступать. Future forms include вступлю and вступит. Вступить в силу has a rule or decision as subject; the future names when it becomes effective. The phrase describes the rules becoming effective after publication. |
| ru-05-0639 | C1g | removed the item from the agenda; Past feminine singular сняла agrees with комиссия; вопрос is accusative and с governs genitive повестки. Снять is perfective; снимать is its imperfective partner. Compare снимет. The frame is снять что с повестки; the past reports a completed removal. The collocation concerns removing an item from consideration at a meeting. |
| ru-05-0640 | C1g | secured additional funds; Past masculine singular привлёк agrees with город; дополнительные средства is accusative plural. Привлечь is perfective; привлекать is its imperfective partner. Compare привлечёт. It takes an accusative object; the past reports securing the funds. Привлечь средства means to obtain funding from additional sources. |
| ru-05-0641 | C1g | carries out; Third-person singular present, agreeing with заместитель. Imperfective; perfective исполнить. Present forms include исполняю and исполняют. It takes an accusative object; the present describes a temporary continuing role. Исполнять обязанности means to perform the duties of a role. |
| ru-05-0642 | C1g | is entitled; Invariable predicative followed by an infinitive. Вправе presents the action as a right available to the customer. |
| ru-05-0643 | C1g | assigned; Feminine singular past, agreeing with комиссия. Perfective; imperfective возлагать. Future forms include возложу and возложит. Возложить что на кого takes an accusative duty and an accusative person; the past reports an assignment. Возложить контроль на кого means to assign oversight to that person. |
| ru-05-0644 | C1g | area of responsibility; Nominative singular feminine сфера is the subject; ответственности is dependent genitive singular. The phrase identifies the domain for which each party is responsible. |
| ru-05-0645 | C1g | legal force; Genitive singular feminine after не имеет; юридической agrees with силы. Не иметь юридической силы describes an agreement that has no legal force. |
| ru-05-0646 | C1g | comply with; Imperfective infinitive after обязан. Imperfective; perfective соблюсти. Present forms include соблюдаю and соблюдает. It takes an accusative rule or condition; the infinitive states an ongoing obligation. Соблюдать условия means to act in accordance with the conditions. |
| ru-05-0647 | C1g | expired; Masculine singular past, agreeing with срок. Perfective; imperfective истекать. Future forms include истечёт and истекут. A period or deadline is the subject; the past reports its expiry. Истечь describes a period reaching its end. |
| ru-05-0648 | C1g | authority to sign; Nominative singular neuter право is the subject; подписи is dependent genitive singular. Право подписи names the authority to sign documents. |
| ru-05-0649 | C1g | pay off the debt; Perfective infinitive after обязана; задолженность is accusative singular feminine. Погасить is perfective; imperfective погашать. Future forms include погашу and погасит. It takes an accusative debt; the infinitive states the obligation to pay it in full. Погасить задолженность means to settle an outstanding debt; перед поставщиком identifies the party owed. |
| ru-05-0650 | C1g | timely; Genitive singular feminine, agreeing with поставки after от. Своевременная поставка is a delivery made at the required time. |
| ru-05-0651 | C1g | spend; Imperfective infinitive after можно. Imperfective; perfective израсходовать emphasizes using up an amount. Present forms include расходую and расходует. Расходовать что на что takes an accusative resource and purpose; the infinitive states permitted use. На цели проекта identifies the permitted purpose of expenditure. |
| ru-05-0652 | C1g | a stock; Nominative singular masculine inanimate; subject of остался. Запас краски is paint kept available for later use. |
| ru-05-0653 | C1g | replenish; Perfective infinitive after позволили. Perfective; imperfective пополнять. Future forms include пополню and пополнит. It takes an accusative stock or resource; the infinitive presents a completed replenishment. Пополнить запас means to add to an existing stock. |
| ru-05-0654 | C1g | amounted to; Feminine singular past, agreeing with стоимость. Perfective; imperfective составлять. Future forms include составлю and составит. In this numerical sense it takes an accusative amount; the past reports the total. Составить половину бюджета states the proportion represented by the rent. |
| ru-05-0655 | C1g | underestimated; Instrumental singular feminine, agreeing with оценка after оказалась. Заниженная оценка is an estimate that is too low. |
| ru-05-0656 | C1g | share; Nominative singular feminine inanimate; subject of уходит. Доля бюджета names a portion of the budget. |
| ru-05-0657 | C1g | costs; Accusative plural inanimate; object of сократить. Издержки refers to costs incurred in an activity. |
| ru-05-0658 | C1g | an earmarked subsidy; Accusative singular feminine; целевую agrees with субсидию, the object of получила. A целевая субсидия is funding designated for a specified purpose, here the purchase of books. |
| ru-05-0659 | C1g | a cash flow gap; Nominative singular masculine; кассовый agrees with разрыв, the subject of возник. Кассовый разрыв is a temporary shortage of available cash caused by a mismatch between incoming and outgoing payments. |
| ru-05-0660 | C1g | unforeseen; Accusative plural, agreeing with расходы after на. Непредвиденные расходы are expenses that were not anticipated. |
| ru-05-0661 | C1g | preferred; Feminine singular past, agreeing with комиссия; предпочтение is accusative singular neuter. Отдать is perfective; imperfective отдавать. Future forms include отдам and отдаст. Отдать предпочтение кому or чему takes the dative; the past reports a completed choice. Местной фирме names the firm chosen in preference to the alternatives. |
| ru-05-0662 | C1g | a compromise between; Instrumental singular masculine компромиссом follows стал; между governs the instrumental. The phrase presents the schedule as a solution balancing two sets of interests. |
| ru-05-0663 | C1g | put to a vote; Masculine singular past; feminine вынесла is also possible because председатель does not establish gender. На governs accusative singular голосование. Вынести is perfective; imperfective выносить. Future forms include вынесу and вынесет. The expression takes an accusative proposal or issue; the past reports its submission for a vote. Два варианта names the options submitted for the members to vote on. |
| ru-05-0664 | C1g | a qualified majority; Nominative singular neuter; квалифицированное agrees with большинство, the subject of требуется. Квалифицированное большинство is a specified threshold greater than a simple majority; the exact threshold depends on the applicable rules. |
| ru-05-0665 | C1g | rejected; Masculine singular past, agreeing with совет. Perfective; imperfective отклонять. Future forms include отклоню and отклонит. It takes an accusative application or proposal; the past reports a completed rejection. Отклонить заявку means to reject the application. |
| ru-05-0666 | C1g | gave consent to; Past plural verb, agreeing with жильцы; согласие is accusative singular neuter, and на governs accusative установку. Дать is perfective; imperfective давать. Future forms include дам and дадут. Дать согласие на takes the accusative of the proposed action; the past reports consent being given. The residents agree to the installation of an entry system. |
| ru-05-0667 | C1g | ratification; Genitive singular feminine after после; парламентом identifies the ratifying body in the instrumental. Ратификация names formal confirmation; парламентом identifies parliament as the ratifying body. |
| ru-05-0668 | C1g | a draft version; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; черновой agrees with вариант, the object of получили. Черновой вариант is an unfinished version prepared for further work or discussion. |
| ru-05-0669 | C1g | a repeat vote; Accusative singular neuter; повторное agrees with голосование, the object of провести. Повторное голосование is another vote held on the matter after an earlier vote. |
| ru-05-0670 | C1g | outweighed; Feminine singular past, agreeing with стоимость. Perfective; imperfective перевешивать. Future forms include перевешу and перевесит. It takes an accusative consideration; the past states the result of the comparison. Перевесить пользу figuratively means to outweigh the benefit in an assessment. |
| ru-05-0671 | C1g | threatens; Third-person singular present, agreeing with отказ. Грозить is imperfective; in this prospective consequence sense it has no simple perfective partner. Present forms include грожу and грозят. Грозить кому чем takes a dative person affected and an instrumental consequence; the present warns of a possible outcome. Жильцам identifies those at risk, and новыми протечками names the anticipated harm. |
| ru-05-0672 | C1g | was idle; Neuter singular past, agreeing with производство. Простаивать is imperfective; perfective простоять can express a completed period of idleness. Present forms include простаивает and простаивают. The idle operation is the subject; the imperfective past describes its duration. The production operation was idle because the delivery was delayed. |
| ru-05-0673 | C1g | under threat of; Под governs instrumental singular feminine угрозой; закрытия is dependent genitive. Под угрозой закрытия means that closure is a danger facing the project. |
| ru-05-0674 | C1g | for safety reasons; Из governs genitive plural соображений; безопасности is dependent genitive singular. The phrase states the safety-related reason for the cancellation. |
| ru-05-0675 | C1g | costly; Genitive singular masculine, agreeing with ремонта after избежать. Дорогостоящий describes something requiring substantial expense. |
| ru-05-0676 | C1g | subject to compliance with; При governs prepositional singular соблюдении; правил is dependent genitive plural. The phrase makes compliance with the rules a condition for access. |
| ru-05-0677 | C1g | failed to honor; Masculine singular past; visible доставил fixes masculine agreement for the same supplier. Perfective; imperfective срывать. Future forms include сорву and сорвёт. It takes an accusative agreement or plan; the past reports a failure to carry it through. Сорвать договорённость means to cause an agreement to fail. |
| ru-05-0678 | C1g | undermined trust; Feminine singular past, agreeing with утечка; доверие is accusative singular neuter. Подорвать is perfective; imperfective подрывать. Future forms include подорву and подорвёт. It takes an accusative object; the past reports the damage to trust caused by the leak. Подорвать доверие figuratively means to weaken trust; клиентов identifies whose trust was affected, and к компании identifies its object. |
| ru-05-0679 | C1g | a risk assessment; Accusative singular feminine оценку is the object of провели; рисков is dependent genitive plural. Оценка рисков is an assessment of potential adverse events; повреждения документов specifies the harm considered. |
| ru-05-0680 | C1g | backup copy; Genitive singular feminine after из; резервной agrees with копии. Резервная копия is a copy kept for recovery if the original data is lost or damaged. |
| ru-05-0681 | C1g | contractual penalty; Nominative singular feminine; subject of предусмотрена. Неустойка is a payment stipulated for failure to meet an obligation. |
| ru-05-0682 | C1g | defective; Accusative singular feminine, agreeing with партию. Бракованная партия contains goods that fail to meet quality requirements. |
| ru-05-0683 | C1g | financial liability; Accusative singular feminine; материальную agrees with ответственность, the object of несёт. Нести материальную ответственность за names financial liability concerning the equipment; за governs the accusative. |
| ru-05-0684 | C1g | handed over duties; Feminine singular past, agreeing with Мария; дела is accusative plural. Передать is perfective; передавать is imperfective. Future forms include передам and передаст. Передать дела кому takes a dative recipient; the past reports a completed handover. Передать дела means to hand over ongoing work and relevant information to another person. |
| ru-05-0685 | C1g | instruction; Nominative singular neuter; subject of требуется. Распоряжение here is an instruction issued by a person with authority. |
| ru-05-0686 | C1g | mandate; Genitive singular masculine after в пределах. Мандат here is the authority given to the commission to perform its work. |
| ru-05-0687 | C1g | mutual checking; Genitive singular masculine after для; взаимного agrees with контроля. Взаимный контроль describes people checking one another's work. |
| ru-05-0688 | C1g | payroll budget; Nominative singular masculine фонд is the subject; оплаты and труда are dependent genitives. Фонд оплаты труда is the money allocated for employees' pay. |
| ru-05-0689 | C1g | revenue; Nominative singular feminine; subject of покрывает. Выручка от продажи is the money received from sales, before costs are deducted. |
| ru-05-0690 | C1g | unaffordable; По governs dative plural средствам; ей identifies the party unable to afford the purchase. Не по средствам describes a cost beyond the available financial means. |
| ru-05-0691 | C1g | through one's own efforts; Instrumental plural; собственными agrees with силами. Собственными силами indicates that the residents will undertake the collection themselves. |
| ru-05-0692 | C1g | further revisions; Accusative plural feminine after на. Доработки are additional changes made to improve or complete work. |
| ru-05-0693 | C1g | exceeds; Third-person singular present, agreeing with объём. Превышать is imperfective; превысить is perfective. Present forms include превышаю and превышают. It takes an accusative limit or capacity; the present states the current relationship. |
| ru-05-0694 | C1g | with a deficit; С governs instrumental singular masculine дефицитом. Бюджет с дефицитом has planned expenditure exceeding planned income. |
| ru-05-0695 | C1g | phased; Accusative singular neuter, agreeing with внедрение. Поэтапное внедрение introduces the system in stages. |
| ru-05-0696 | C1g | acceptance of applications; Accusative singular masculine приём is the object of продлить; заявок is dependent genitive plural. Приём заявок names the period or process of accepting applications. |
| ru-05-0697 | C1g | casting vote; Nominative singular masculine; решающий agrees with голос, the subject of принадлежит. Решающий голос gives the chair the deciding vote when the votes are tied. |
| ru-05-0698 | C1g | backup supplier; Nominative singular masculine; запасной agrees with поставщик, the subject of обеспечит. The supplier is available as an alternative if the expected delivery is delayed. |
| ru-05-0699 | C1g | losses; Genitive plural masculine after избежать. Убытки here are financial losses that might have resulted from the error. |
| ru-05-0700 | C1g | overspending; Accusative singular masculine перерасход is the object of обнаружила; средств is dependent genitive plural. Перерасход средств means expenditure beyond the amount allocated or planned. |
| ru-05-0701 | C1h | thesis; Nominative singular masculine; subject of сформулирован. Тезис о introduces a proposition concerning the topic named after о. |
| ru-05-0702 | C1h | conventional nature; Accusative singular feminine; object of признают. Условность деления indicates that the division is a convention rather than an absolute boundary. |
| ru-05-0703 | C1h | the discrepancy between the forecast and; Расхождение is accusative singular neuter; прогноза is genitive singular. С governs the following instrumental phrase. The construction identifies a discrepancy between a forecast and the observed data. |
| ru-05-0704 | C1h | whether robust; Short masculine singular adjective agreeing with эффект; ли introduces the indirect question. The construction states the content of the unresolved question about the effect. |
| ru-05-0705 | C1h | the possibility that; Accusative singular feminine возможность is the object of обсуждают; того, что introduces its content. The construction presents the following proposition as a possibility under discussion. |
| ru-05-0706 | C1h | completeness; Accusative singular feminine; object of ставит под вопрос. Полнота сведений concerns whether the information covers all relevant material. |
| ru-05-0707 | C1h | justification; Nominative singular neuter; subject of приведено. Обоснование выбора gives reasons for the choice of method. |
| ru-05-0708 | C1h | sample representativeness; Accusative singular feminine репрезентативность is the object of оценивают; выборки is dependent genitive singular. The phrase concerns how well the sample represents the population; по возрасту identifies the characteristic assessed. |
| ru-05-0709 | C1h | as to whom; О governs prepositional том; dative кому introduces the indirect question. The construction attaches an indirect question to вопрос. |
| ru-05-0710 | C1h | the limitations of the data for; Ограниченность is accusative singular feminine after на; данных is genitive plural. Для governs the following genitive phrase. The construction relates a limitation in the data to the proposed use of those data. |
| ru-05-0711 | C1h | about the purposes of visits; О governs prepositional plural целях; визитов is dependent genitive plural. Сведения о introduces the subject about which information is absent. |
| ru-05-0712 | C1h | insufficient clarity; Accusative singular feminine; недостаточную agrees with ясность, the object of отмечают. The authors identify a lack of clarity in the definition. |
| ru-05-0713 | C1h | about the constancy; О governs prepositional singular neuter постоянстве. Допущение о introduces the content of the assumption; условий names what is assumed constant. |
| ru-05-0714 | C1h | without specifying; Без governs genitive singular neuter указания. Без указания introduces information omitted from the description. |
| ru-05-0715 | C1h | confidence that; Genitive singular feminine уверенности depends on степень; в governs prepositional том. Уверенность в том, что attaches the proposition in the following clause to the noun. |
| ru-05-0716 | C1h | on which; От governs genitive того; какие agrees with plural ответы in the indirect question. Зависеть от того, какие connects the result to the choice of answers included. |
| ru-05-0717 | C1h | a weakness; Instrumental singular feminine, predicative after называют. Назвать что чем characterizes the lack of repeat measurements as a weakness. |
| ru-05-0718 | C1h | supporters and critics; Genitive plural masculine animate nouns, dependent on аргументы. The genitives identify the people whose arguments are examined. |
| ru-05-0719 | C1h | how; Instrumental singular masculine образом with agreeing каким. The phrase introduces an indirect question about the way participants chose sources. |
| ru-05-0720 | C1h | as grounds for; Accusative singular neuter основание follows как; для governs genitive проверки. Рассматривать что как основание для presents the results as grounds for the specified further action. |
| ru-05-0721 | C1h | the probability that; Accusative singular feminine вероятность is the object of признают; того, что introduces its propositional content. The construction presents the following proposition in terms of its probability. |
| ru-05-0722 | C1h | on the premise; На governs prepositional singular feminine предпосылке. Основан на identifies the premise on which the conclusion rests; the following clause gives its content. |
| ru-05-0723 | C1h | about when; О governs prepositional том; когда introduces an indirect question about time. Сведения о том, когда attaches the indirect question to the information needed. |
| ru-05-0724 | C1h | applicability of the method to; Accusative singular feminine применимость is the object of обсуждают; метода is dependent genitive singular, and к governs dative данным. Применимость чего к чему identifies both the method and the data to which it may be applicable. |
| ru-05-0725 | C1h | to a shortage; На governs accusative singular feminine нехватку. Указывать на нехватку чего identifies a shortage; данных names what is lacking. |
| ru-05-0726 | C1h | accuracy; Nominative singular feminine; subject of зависит. Точность оценки concerns the accuracy of the estimate; оценки identifies what is described as accurate. |
| ru-05-0727 | C1h | as to why; О governs prepositional том; почему introduces an indirect question about cause. The construction attaches the indirect question to вопрос. |
| ru-05-0728 | C1h | on what grounds; На governs prepositional singular neuter основании; каком agrees with it. The indirect question asks for the grounds for excluding the answers. |
| ru-05-0729 | C1h | persuasiveness; Accusative singular feminine; direct object of снижает. Убедительность объяснения concerns how persuasive the explanation is. |
| ru-05-0730 | C1h | absence of a response; Accusative singular neuter отсутствие is an object of различают; ответа is dependent genitive singular. The report distinguishes a missing response from refusal to participate. |
| ru-05-0731 | C1h | the assumption of independence; Допущении is prepositional singular neuter after на; независимости is genitive singular feminine. Независимость наблюдений names the property assumed as a basis for the conclusion. |
| ru-05-0732 | C1h | reliability of measurements; Nominative singular feminine надёжность is the subject of зависит; измерений is dependent genitive plural. The phrase concerns the reliability of the measurements. |
| ru-05-0733 | C1h | in such a form that; В governs prepositional singular masculine виде; таком agrees with it, and чтобы introduces the intended result. The construction links the form of presentation to the reader's ability to check the calculations. |
| ru-05-0734 | C1h | disproportion; Accusative singular feminine; direct object of отмечает. Несоразмерность чего чему identifies the conclusion as disproportionate to the data. |
| ru-05-0735 | C1h | to what extent; В governs prepositional singular feminine мере; какой agrees with it. The phrase introduces an indirect question about the degree of dependence. |
| ru-05-0736 | C1h | plausibility; Accusative singular neuter; direct object of обсуждают. Правдоподобие объяснения concerns whether the explanation is plausible; it does not establish proof. |
| ru-05-0737 | C1h | when interpreting; При governs prepositional singular feminine интерпретации. При интерпретации ответов identifies the activity during which the differences should be considered. |
| ru-05-0738 | C1h | on how; От governs genitive того; насколько introduces an indirect question about degree. Зависеть от того, насколько links the conclusion's reliability to the degree of completeness. |
| ru-05-0739 | C1h | heterogeneity of the group; Accusative singular feminine неоднородность is the object of отмечают; группы is dependent genitive singular. The stated age range illustrates a respect in which the group is heterogeneous. |
| ru-05-0740 | C1h | consistency of dates; Accusative singular feminine согласованность is the object of проверить; дат is dependent genitive plural. The phrase concerns whether the dates in the documents are consistent with each other. |
| ru-05-0741 | C1h | about intentions; О governs prepositional plural neuter намерениях. Данные о introduces the subject of the data, contrasted with actual actions. |
| ru-05-0742 | C1h | comparability of definitions; Genitive singular feminine сопоставимости follows от; определений is dependent genitive plural. Сопоставимость определений concerns whether the definitions can be meaningfully compared. |
| ru-05-0743 | C1h | the result's sensitivity to; Чувствительность is accusative singular feminine; результата is genitive singular. К governs the following dative phrase. The construction relates the result to a change that may affect it. |
| ru-05-0744 | C1h | by comparing; Путём governs genitive singular neuter сопоставления. The phrase identifies the method used to obtain the estimate; ответов names what was compared. |
| ru-05-0745 | C1h | to a contradiction between; На governs accusative singular neuter противоречие; между governs the following instrumental nouns. Обращать внимание на identifies the issue being highlighted; между names its two sides. |
| ru-05-0746 | C1h | participants' refusal to be interviewed; Accusative singular masculine отказ is the object of рассматривают; участников identifies the people refusing, and от governs genitive интервью. Отказ от интервью names the activity the participants decline. |
| ru-05-0747 | C1h | temporal coverage; Nominative singular masculine охват is the subject of ограничен; временной agrees with it. Временной охват опроса identifies the time covered by the survey; будними днями specifies its limit. |
| ru-05-0748 | C1h | whether there is enough data; Достаточно is an impersonal predicative; ли marks the indirect question and данных is genitive plural. The construction asks whether the available data suffice for the stated assessment. |
| ru-05-0749 | C1h | permissibility; Nominative singular feminine; subject of оговорена. Допустимость повторного использования concerns whether the records may be reused. |
| ru-05-0750 | C1h | by the fact that; Instrumental тем links обоснован to the following что-clause. Обоснован тем, что introduces the reason given for the choice. |
| ru-05-0751 | C1h | about availability; О governs prepositional singular feminine доступности. Вопрос о доступности introduces the issue of whether the records are available to other researchers. |
| ru-05-0752 | C1h | for inclusion; За governs accusative singular neuter включение. Доводы за включение are arguments in favor of including the specified data. |
| ru-05-0753 | C1h | self-selection; Genitive singular masculine, dependent on риск. Риск самоотбора identifies the risk arising when people select themselves into a study; here participation was voluntary. |
| ru-05-0754 | C1h | justification for why; Genitive singular neuter обоснования depends on отсутствие; того, почему introduces the indirect question. The construction identifies the missing justification for the choice. |
| ru-05-0755 | C1h | dependence of the estimate on; Accusative singular feminine зависимость is the object of признают; оценки is dependent genitive, and от governs the following genitive. Зависимость чего от чего identifies both the estimate that depends and the method it depends on. |
| ru-05-0756 | C1h | exactly which; Nominative plural какие agrees with записи; именно specifies the focus of the indirect question. The phrase asks for the particular records included in the analysis. |
| ru-05-0757 | C1h | unequal duration; Accusative singular feminine; неодинаковую agrees with продолжительность after на. The phrase identifies the unequal lengths of observation for the two groups. |
| ru-05-0758 | C1h | for what reason; По governs dative singular feminine причине; какой agrees with it. The phrase introduces an indirect question about the reason for the records being unavailable. |
| ru-05-0759 | C1h | after exclusion; После governs genitive singular neuter исключения. The phrase locates the remaining difference after the specified answers have been excluded. |
| ru-05-0760 | C1h | from their interpretation; От governs genitive singular feminine интерпретации; их refers to результаты. Отделить что от чего identifies the two things the reviewer asks the author to distinguish. |
| ru-05-0761 | C1h | by which; По governs dative singular masculine которому, referring to критерий. The relative construction identifies the criterion used to classify the answers. |
| ru-05-0762 | C1h | the risk that; Accusative singular masculine риск is the object of обсуждают; того, что introduces its propositional content. The construction presents the following event as a risk. |
| ru-05-0763 | C1h | incompleteness of the archive; Nominative singular feminine неполнота is the subject of признана; архива is dependent genitive singular. The missing March records illustrate the archive's incompleteness. |
| ru-05-0764 | C1h | without a breakdown; Без governs genitive singular feminine разбивки. По районам identifies the categories missing from the presentation of the data. |
| ru-05-0765 | C1h | justification for extending the conclusions; Accusative singular feminine обоснованность is the object of ставит под вопрос; переноса and выводов are dependent genitives. На другие регионы identifies where the conclusions would be applied; the phrase questions whether that extension is justified. |
| ru-05-0766 | C1h | the method's vulnerability to; Accusative singular feminine уязвимость is the object of отмечают; метода is genitive, and к governs dative ошибкам. The construction names the method and the kind of error to which it is vulnerable. |
| ru-05-0767 | C1h | for how long; На governs accusative singular masculine срок; какой agrees with it. The indirect question asks for the period for which the devices were issued. |
| ru-05-0768 | C1h | interim; Genitive plural adjective agreeing with результатов. The adjective identifies results reported before the study is complete. |
| ru-05-0769 | C1h | an explanation of how; Nominative singular neuter объяснение is the subject of отсутствует; того, как introduces an indirect question. The construction identifies the missing explanation of the procedure. |
| ru-05-0770 | C1h | about the distinction; О governs prepositional singular neuter разграничении. Вопрос о разграничении introduces the issue of separating the two named kinds of material. |
