| ru-05-0771 | C1h | elimination of systematic errors; Accusative singular neuter устранение is the object of обсуждают; систематических ошибок is dependent genitive plural. Ошибок identifies what the authors discuss eliminating before processing the data. |
| ru-05-0772 | C1h | recalculation of the indicators; Genitive singular masculine пересчёта is governed by потребовало; показателей is dependent genitive plural. Показателей identifies the values recalculated because the units changed. |
| ru-05-0773 | C1h | the term's multiple meanings; Accusative singular feminine многозначность is the object of отмечают; термина is dependent genitive singular. The following clause shows that the same term denotes different phenomena in different articles. |
| ru-05-0774 | C1h | about repeat requests; О governs prepositional plural обращениях; повторных agrees with it. The phrase concerns repeat requests; в клинику за медицинской помощью identifies requests to the clinic for medical care, without specifying whether they involved visits. |
| ru-05-0775 | C1h | limits of generalization; Accusative plural masculine пределы is the object of обсуждают; обобщения is dependent genitive singular. The student-only sample gives a reason to consider the limits of generalization. |
| ru-05-0776 | C1h | loss of data; Genitive singular feminine утраты depends on последствия; данных is dependent genitive plural. Данных identifies the data whose loss is discussed; при сбое сервера identifies the circumstances in which the loss could occur. |
| ru-05-0777 | C1h | from what; Из governs genitive чего. Складываться из чего introduces the components making up the overall assessment. |
| ru-05-0778 | C1h | the limited scope of the conclusions; Accusative singular feminine ограниченность is the object of отмечают; выводов is dependent genitive plural. The single interview identifies the narrow basis of the conclusions. |
| ru-05-0779 | C1h | whether one should; Third-person singular present следует is used impersonally; ли marks the indirect question. Следовать is imperfective, with no simple perfective partner in this impersonal sense. Compare следовало. Следует plus an infinitive expresses what should be done; the present states the current question. The construction asks whether repeating the measurements is advisable. |
| ru-05-0780 | C1h | grounds for the choice; Nominative plural neuter основания is the subject of приведены; для governs genitive singular выбора. Именно этого периода specifies the choice for which grounds are given. |
| ru-05-0781 | C1h | the possibility of a repeat survey; Accusative singular feminine возможность is the object of рассматривают; повторного опроса is genitive singular. Участников identifies the people who would be surveyed again. |
| ru-05-0782 | C1h | on which sources; На governs accusative plural источники; какие agrees with it. Опираться на что introduces the sources underlying the forecast. |
| ru-05-0783 | C1h | incomplete correspondence; Accusative singular neuter совпадение follows на; неполное agrees with it. Категорий identifies what corresponds only in part across the questionnaires. |
| ru-05-0784 | C1h | whether available; Short feminine singular adjective доступна agrees with запись; ли marks the indirect question. От того, доступна ли links the choice of method to the availability of the recording. |
| ru-05-0785 | C1h | assessment of translation quality; Accusative singular feminine оценку is the object of отделить; качества and перевода are dependent genitives. Качества names what is assessed, and перевода identifies the work whose quality is assessed. |
| ru-05-0786 | C1h | below the required level; The comparative ниже takes genitive feminine требуемой, a present passive participle used adjectivally and referring to the required accuracy. Требовать is imperfective; there is no simple perfective substitution for the continuing requirement here. Потребовать describes an act of making a demand. First conjugation; compare требует. It takes an accusative object; the present passive participle describes the accuracy required for the measurement. The construction compares the instrument's accuracy with the accuracy required for the measurement. |
| ru-05-0787 | C1h | the validity of the comparison; Правомерность is nominative singular feminine; сравнения is genitive singular neuter. The phrase makes the validity of comparing the groups the subject of discussion. |
| ru-05-0788 | C1h | about those who; О governs prepositional plural тех; кто introduces the relative clause. The construction identifies the people about whom information is absent. |
| ru-05-0789 | C1h | similarity of the mean values; Nominative singular neuter сходство is the subject of не исключает; средних значений is genitive plural. The sentence distinguishes similarity between means from variation within groups. |
| ru-05-0790 | C1h | the basis for the forecast; Instrumental singular feminine основой follows послужили; для governs genitive singular прогноза. Послужить основой для identifies what provides the basis for the forecast. |
| ru-05-0791 | C1h | the group of potential beneficiaries; Accusative singular masculine круг is the object of ограничивают; потенциальных бенефициаров is dependent genitive plural. Бенефициары are those who may benefit from the program; сельскими школами specifies the institutions to which the authors limit that group. |
| ru-05-0792 | C1h | the mixed nature of the results; Accusative singular feminine неоднозначность is the object of признают; результатов is dependent genitive plural. The different directions of change explain why the results are described as mixed. |
| ru-05-0793 | C1h | of which; Genitive того depends on указания; nominative plural какие agrees with расходы. Указание того, какие attaches the indirect question to the information omitted. |
| ru-05-0794 | C1h | the consequences of the fact that; Accusative plural neuter последствия is the object of обсуждают; того, что introduces the event whose consequences are discussed. The construction connects the consequences to the unavailability of some records. |
| ru-05-0795 | C1h | between data and assumptions; Между governs the instrumental plurals данными and предположениями. Разграничение между identifies the two categories that should be distinguished. |
| ru-05-0796 | C1h | outside the laboratory; За governs instrumental plural пределами; лаборатории is dependent genitive singular. The phrase identifies the setting in which the model's application is being considered: outside the laboratory. |
| ru-05-0797 | C1h | for whom; У governs genitive plural которых, referring to участников. The relative clause identifies participants for whom repeat measurements are missing. |
| ru-05-0798 | C1h | independent verification; Dative singular feminine проверке is governed by препятствует; независимой agrees with it. Расчётов identifies what would be independently checked. |
| ru-05-0799 | C1h | whether in sufficient detail; The particle ли marks an indirect question; достаточно modifies the adverb подробно. The construction asks whether the description provides enough detail. |
| ru-05-0800 | C1h | for the cases examined; Для governs genitive plural случаев; рассмотренных is a past passive participle agreeing with it. Рассмотреть is perfective; imperfective рассматривать. Compare the future form рассмотрит. It takes an accusative object; the past passive participle describes cases already examined. The phrase identifies the cases to which the conclusion applies; только limits its scope. |
| ru-05-0801 | C1i | incoming registration number; Accusative singular masculine; object of сообщить. The number identifies an incoming item in the recipient's records. |
| ru-05-0802 | C1i | cover letter; Accusative singular neuter; object of приложите. A сопроводительное письмо accompanies the documents and explains their purpose. |
| ru-05-0803 | C1i | applicant's signature; Nominative singular feminine подпись is the subject of отсутствует; заявителя is genitive singular. |
| ru-05-0804 | C1i | outgoing registration number; Accusative singular masculine; object of укажите. The number identifies the reply in the sending organization's outgoing correspondence. |
| ru-05-0805 | C1i | period for consideration; Nominative singular masculine срок is the subject of указан; рассмотрения is genitive singular. Повторного обращения identifies the matter to be considered. |
| ru-05-0806 | C1i | reasoned refusal; Accusative singular masculine; object of предоставить. A мотивированный отказ states the reasons for refusing. |
| ru-05-0807 | C1i | we inform you; First-person plural present доводим; до governs genitive singular вашего сведения. Imperfective доводить; perfective довести. Compare доведём. The frame доводить до сведения кого introduces the information with что; the present conveys the notice in this letter. The target imperfective conjugates «доводит» (second conjugation). Recognition: this formal correspondence formula communicates information to the addressee. |
| ru-05-0808 | C1i | according to the procedure; Согласно governs dative singular регламенту. The phrase attributes the obligation to the stated procedure. |
| ru-05-0809 | C1i | under a power of attorney; По governs dative singular доверенности. The phrase identifies the authorization under which the representative acts. |
| ru-05-0810 | C1i | has been suspended; Short past passive participle, neuter singular, agreeing with предоставление. Perfective приостановить; imperfective приостанавливать. Compare приостановит. The active verb takes an accusative object; the participle presents the provision as suspended. До получения marks the point until which the suspension lasts. |
| ru-05-0811 | C1i | withdraw an application; Perfective infinitive followed by an accusative singular object. Perfective отозвать; imperfective отзывать. Compare отзовёт. It takes an accusative object; after может the infinitive expresses an available action. До принятия решения sets the stated time limit for withdrawal. |
| ru-05-0812 | C1i | deficiencies in the application; Accusative plural недостатки is the object of устранить; заявления is dependent genitive singular. The phrase refers to defects to be corrected in the application. |
| ru-05-0813 | C1i | rescheduling an appointment; Genitive singular masculine переноса follows для; приёма is dependent genitive singular. Перенос приёма refers to changing the scheduled time of an appointment. |
| ru-05-0814 | C1i | of the decision made; О governs prepositional singular решении; принятом agrees with it. Принять is perfective; imperfective принимать. Compare примет. It takes an accusative object; the past passive participle describes a decision already made. Уведомить кого о чём names the recipient in the accusative and the subject of notification after о. |
| ru-05-0815 | C1i | on condition of; The compound preposition governs genitive предварительной записи. It introduces a condition for acceptance, rather than a suggestion. |
| ru-05-0816 | C1i | authorized representative; Accusative singular masculine animate after через; уполномоченного agrees with представителя. Уполномочить is perfective; imperfective уполномочивать. Compare уполномочит. It takes an accusative person; the participle describes a person who has been authorized. По доверенности specifies the representative's authorization. |
| ru-05-0817 | C1i | within; The compound preposition governs genitive пяти рабочих дней. With a stated period, it sets the interval within which the reply will be sent. |
| ru-05-0818 | C1i | appeal procedure; Accusative singular masculine порядок is the object of разъяснила; обжалования is dependent genitive singular. The phrase refers to the procedure for challenging a decision; своего решения identifies the decision. |
| ru-05-0819 | C1i | without consideration; Без governs genitive singular рассмотрения. Оставить заявление без рассмотрения means not to consider the application; the sentence gives the reason. |
| ru-05-0820 | C1i | in accordance with the established procedure; В governs prepositional singular порядке; установленном agrees with it. Установить is perfective; imperfective устанавливать. Compare установит. It takes an accusative object; the past passive participle describes a procedure already established. The phrase refers to the applicable procedure without specifying its details. |
| ru-05-0821 | C1i | confirming; Present active participle, accusative plural inanimate, agreeing with документы. Imperfective подтверждать; perfective подтвердить. Compare подтверждает. It takes an accusative object; the participle identifies documents that confirm the information. Controlled productive written use: the participial phrase specifies which documents are requested. |
| ru-05-0822 | C1i | in electronic form; В governs prepositional singular форме; электронной agrees with it. The phrase specifies the requested form of the reply. |
| ru-05-0823 | C1i | completeness of the documents; Accusative singular feminine комплектность is the object of проверит; документов is dependent genitive plural. Комплектность concerns whether the required set is complete. |
| ru-05-0824 | C1i | duplicate; Genitive singular masculine, dependent on получения. Справки identifies the document for which a duplicate is requested. |
| ru-05-0825 | C1i | of receipt of the documents; О governs prepositional singular приёме; документов is dependent genitive plural. Расписка о приёме документов acknowledges that the documents were received. |
| ru-05-0826 | C1i | by the director's order; Instrumental singular приказом identifies the instrument establishing the procedure; директора is dependent genitive singular. The phrase identifies the formal order establishing the procedure. |
| ru-05-0827 | C1i | after obtaining agreement; После governs genitive singular согласования. С его владельцем identifies the party whose agreement is sought. |
| ru-05-0828 | C1i | change of address; Genitive singular neuter изменения depends on в случае; адреса is dependent genitive singular. В случае marks a change of address as the condition for the request. |
| ru-05-0829 | C1i | hereby; Recognition: this official formula marks the confirmation as being made by the present communication. |
| ru-05-0830 | C1i | in full; В governs prepositional singular объёме; полном agrees with it. The phrase asks for the information in its entirety. |
| ru-05-0831 | C1i | is subject to registration; Third-person singular present подлежит governs dative singular регистрации. Подлежать is imperfective with no simple aspectual partner in this obligation frame; compare подлежат. It governs a dative verbal noun, and the present states the procedural requirement. Controlled production: подлежать регистрации states a requirement, not a recommendation. |
| ru-05-0832 | C1i | we recommend; First-person plural present. Рекомендовать is imperfective in this ongoing recommendation; порекомендовать can express a particular completed recommendation. Compare рекомендует. It introduces an infinitive; the present makes a recommendation. Рекомендуем advises the reader to check; it does not state an obligation. |
| ru-05-0833 | C1i | is required; Short masculine singular form, agreeing grammatically with заявитель; it governs the infinitive предъявить. Обязан expresses an obligation attributed here to the centre's rules. |
| ru-05-0834 | C1i | to the responsible office; По governs dative singular принадлежности. Recognition: the formula describes forwarding a matter to the office responsible for it; the clause identifies that office. |
| ru-05-0835 | C1i | list of documents; Accusative singular masculine перечень is the object of содержит; документов is dependent genitive plural. Перечень presents an enumerated set of items. |
| ru-05-0836 | C1i | certify; Perfective infinitive governed by просим. Perfective заверить; imperfective заверять. Compare заверит. It takes an accusative object; the request calls for certification of the copy. Заверить копию means to certify a copy; подписью specifies the means in this request. |
| ru-05-0837 | C1i | grounds for refusal; Instrumental singular neuter основанием follows послужило; для governs genitive singular отказа. The sentence identifies the absence of a required document as the grounds for refusal. |
| ru-05-0838 | C1i | no later than; The comparative expression precedes a genitive date. It sets the latest acceptable date for sending the information. |
| ru-05-0839 | C1i | from the moment of registration; С governs genitive singular момента; регистрации is dependent genitive singular. The phrase identifies registration as the event from which the application is considered submitted. |
| ru-05-0840 | C1i | following the review; По governs dative plural итогам; проверки is dependent genitive singular. The phrase links the commission's decision to the outcome of the review. |
| ru-05-0841 | C1i | results of consideration; Accusative plural inanimate результаты is the object of сообщить; рассмотрения is dependent genitive singular. Моего заявления identifies the application whose consideration produced the results. |
| ru-05-0842 | C1i | confirmation of identity; Genitive singular подтверждения follows для; личности is dependent genitive singular. Личности identifies what the passport copy is intended to confirm. |
| ru-05-0843 | C1i | method of receipt; Accusative singular masculine способ is the object of указать; получения is dependent genitive singular. Готовой справки identifies the document to be received. |
| ru-05-0844 | C1i | contact details; Accusative plural inanimate; object of указать. The phrase names the information needed to contact the applicant. |
| ru-05-0845 | C1i | date of receipt; Accusative singular feminine дату is the object of укажите; поступления is dependent genitive singular. Заявления identifies the item received by the department. |
| ru-05-0846 | C1i | for comparison; Для governs genitive singular сверки. Сверка с копией means checking the original against the copy. |
| ru-05-0847 | C1i | is returned; Third-person singular present passive reflexive form, agreeing with оригинал. Возвращается is the imperfective passive of возвращать; the contextual perfective active counterpart is вернуть. Compare passive возвращаются and active вернёт. The returned item is the subject, and the recipient is dative; the present states a procedure. The original is the item returned; заявителю identifies its recipient. |
| ru-05-0848 | C1i | reissue the certificate; Perfective infinitive followed by accusative singular feminine справку. Переоформить is perfective; imperfective переоформлять. Compare переофо́рмит. It takes an accusative object; after прошу the infinitive requests a completed reissue. The request asks for the certificate to be issued again because it contains an error. |
| ru-05-0849 | C1i | application for a correction; Accusative singular neuter заявление is the object of подайте; об governs prepositional singular исправлении. The phrase names the application requesting a correction; записи identifies what is to be corrected. |
| ru-05-0850 | C1i | registration number; Nominative singular masculine; subject of указан. Его refers to the registered request. |
| ru-05-0851 | C1i | mandatory condition; Nominative singular neuter; обязательное agrees with условие in the nominal predicate. Выдачи архивной справки names the action for which presenting the original is a mandatory condition. |
| ru-05-0852 | C1i | when submitting in person; При governs prepositional singular подаче; личной agrees with it. Заявления names what is submitted; the phrase states when the instruction applies. |
| ru-05-0853 | C1i | certifying; Present active participle, accusative singular masculine inanimate, agreeing with документ. Imperfective удостоверять; perfective удостоверить. Compare удостоверяет. It takes an accusative object; the participle describes the document's identifying function. Controlled productive written use: документ, удостоверяющий личность identifies an identity document. |
| ru-05-0854 | C1i | requested; Past passive participle, accusative plural inanimate, agreeing with сведения. Perfective запросить; imperfective запрашивать. Compare запросит. The active verb takes an accusative object; the participle presents the information as already requested. Controlled productive written use: вашим письмом identifies the communication containing the request. |
| ru-05-0855 | C1i | provide clarification; Perfective infinitive followed by accusative plural inanimate разъяснения. Дать is perfective; давать is its imperfective partner. Compare даст. It takes an accusative object; after прошу the infinitive requests a completed response. О порядке доступа identifies the subject on which clarification is requested. |
| ru-05-0856 | C1i | in connection with; The compound preposition governs instrumental вашим обращением. The phrase links the communication to the addressee's request. |
| ru-05-0857 | C1i | in addition to; The compound preposition governs dative singular письму. The new communication supplements the earlier letter. |
| ru-05-0858 | C1i | reply message; Instrumental singular neuter; ответным agrees with сообщением. The instrumental identifies the requested means of confirming receipt. |
| ru-05-0859 | C1i | reason for return; Accusative singular feminine причину is the object of разъяснить; возврата is dependent genitive singular. Документов names what was returned. |
| ru-05-0860 | C1i | sender; Dative singular masculine animate; identifies the recipient of the returned letter. The sender is the person to whom the undelivered letter returned. |
| ru-05-0861 | C1i | addressee; Accusative singular masculine animate; object of укажите. Адресат is the intended recipient of the letter. |
| ru-05-0862 | C1i | is enclosed; Third-person singular present passive reflexive form, agreeing with копия. Прилагается is the imperfective passive of прилагать; the contextual perfective active counterpart is приложить. Compare passive прилагаются and active приложит. К with the dative names the document accompanied; the enclosed item is the subject, and the present describes the enclosure. The copy accompanies the letter. |
| ru-05-0863 | C1i | enclosures; Accusative plural neuter inanimate; object of перечислите. Here приложения are documents accompanying the letter. |
| ru-05-0864 | C1i | details of the representative; Accusative plural сведения is the object of указать; о governs prepositional singular представителе. The phrase names the information required in the application. |
| ru-05-0865 | C1i | legal representative; Nominative singular masculine animate; subject of может подать. Несовершеннолетнего identifies the person represented. |
| ru-05-0866 | C1i | about the progress of consideration; О governs prepositional singular ходе; рассмотрения is dependent genitive singular. Моего обращения identifies the matter being considered. |
| ru-05-0867 | C1i | under consideration; На governs prepositional singular рассмотрении. Находиться на рассмотрении describes a matter currently being considered. |
| ru-05-0868 | C1i | for consideration; К governs dative singular рассмотрению. Принять заявление к рассмотрению means to accept it for substantive consideration. |
| ru-05-0869 | C1i | with a complaint; С governs instrumental singular жалобой. Обратиться с жалобой names the type of submission made to the commission. |
| ru-05-0870 | C1i | copy of the decision; Accusative singular feminine копию is the object of приложите; решения is dependent genitive singular. The relative clause identifies the decision as the one the applicant wishes to challenge. |
| ru-05-0871 | C1i | submission of a complaint; Genitive singular feminine подачи follows для; жалобы is dependent genitive singular. Жалобы identifies what is submitted. |
| ru-05-0872 | C1i | circumstances; Accusative plural neuter inanimate; object of укажите. The relative clause identifies the circumstances as those invoked in the complaint. |
| ru-05-0873 | C1i | additional materials; Accusative plural inanimate; object of запросить. The phrase identifies further material the commission may request. |
| ru-05-0874 | C1i | if the originals are present; При governs prepositional singular наличии; оригиналов is dependent genitive plural. Только makes the presence of originals a condition of acceptance under the stated rules. |
| ru-05-0875 | C1i | by prior appointment; По governs dative singular записи; предварительной agrees with it. The phrase states the appointment arrangement specified by the centre's rules. |
| ru-05-0876 | C1i | at the appointed time; В governs accusative singular время; назначенное agrees with it. Назначить is perfective; imperfective назначать. Compare назначит. The active verb takes an accusative object; the past passive participle describes a time already set. The phrase specifies when the addressee is asked to attend. |
| ru-05-0877 | C1i | notify the reception office; Perfective infinitive followed by accusative singular feminine приёмную; приёмная is the noun for a reception office. Уведомить is perfective; imperfective уведомлять. Compare уведоми́т. It takes an accusative recipient; after просим the infinitive requests a completed notification. The visitor is asked to notify the reception office in advance of a prospective inability to attend. |
| ru-05-0878 | C1i | agree on a new time; Perfective infinitive followed by accusative singular neuter время; новое agrees with it. Согласовать is perfective; imperfective согласовывать. Compare согласу́ет. It takes an accusative object; after прошу the infinitive requests that agreement be reached. Приёма identifies the appointment whose new time is to be agreed by telephone. |
| ru-05-0879 | C1i | is extended; Third-person singular present passive reflexive form, agreeing with срок. Продлевается is the imperfective passive of продлевать; the contextual perfective active counterpart is продлить. Compare passive продлеваются and active продлит. The extended period is the subject; the active verb takes an accusative object, and the present states the procedure under the specified condition. The extension is attributed to the stated procedure. |
| ru-05-0880 | C1i | about the extension of the period; О governs prepositional singular продлении; срока is dependent genitive singular. Ответа identifies the period being extended; the phrase gives the subject of the notification. |
| ru-05-0881 | C1i | confirming the absence of outstanding debt; Об governs prepositional singular отсутствии; задолженности is dependent genitive singular. Справка об отсутствии задолженности names a certificate confirming that no debt is outstanding. |
| ru-05-0882 | C1i | payment details; Accusative plural inanimate; object of указать. Для перечисления средств identifies the details needed to transfer funds. |
| ru-05-0883 | C1i | delay in issuing; Genitive singular задержки depends on причину; выдачи is dependent genitive singular. Готовой справки identifies the document whose issue is delayed. |
| ru-05-0884 | C1i | position; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; object of указать. Лица identifies the person whose position is requested. |
| ru-05-0885 | C1i | for review; Для governs genitive singular ознакомления. The phrase states the purpose of access: examining the materials. |
| ru-05-0886 | C1i | basis for charging; Accusative singular основание is the object of указать; начисления is dependent genitive singular. Платы identifies the charge whose basis is requested. |
| ru-05-0887 | C1i | written confirmation; Accusative singular neuter; письменное agrees with подтверждение, the object of направить. Отмены записи identifies what the requested confirmation should confirm. |
| ru-05-0888 | C1i | change of surname; Genitive singular смены depends on в случае; фамилии is dependent genitive singular. В случае presents the change as the condition for the request. |
| ru-05-0889 | C1i | grounds for the request; Genitive singular основания depends on указания; обращения is dependent genitive singular. The phrase identifies the grounds the applicant is to enter in the form. |
| ru-05-0890 | C1i | on the merits; По governs dative singular существу. Ответ по существу addresses the substance of the questions. |
| ru-05-0891 | C1i | attachment; Nominative singular neuter; subject of не открывается. В письме identifies the attachment as part of the message. |
| ru-05-0892 | C1i | certification of a copy's fidelity; Nominative singular neuter свидетельствование is the subject of требуется; верности and копии are dependent genitive singular nouns. Нотариальное specifies the notarial procedure; договора identifies the document copied. |
| ru-05-0893 | C1i | deadline for correction; Nominative singular срок is the subject of указан; устранения is dependent genitive singular. Выявленных нарушений identifies what must be corrected within the stated period. |
| ru-05-0894 | C1i | without payment; Без governs genitive singular оплаты. Услуги identifies what does not have to be paid for before the application is accepted. |
| ru-05-0895 | C1i | postal address; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after на; почтовый agrees with адрес. На identifies the destination to which the reply is to be sent. |
| ru-05-0896 | C1i | provision of the rules; Accusative singular пункт is the object of указать; правил is dependent genitive plural. Конкретный asks the recipient to identify a specific provision. |
| ru-05-0897 | C1i | that the certificate is ready; О governs prepositional singular готовности; справки is dependent genitive singular. Сообщить о готовности asks for notification when the certificate is ready. |
| ru-05-0898 | C1i | clerical error; Accusative singular feminine; техническую agrees with ошибку, the object of исправить. В номере договора locates the error. |
| ru-05-0899 | C1i | extract from the register; Accusative singular выписку is the object of предоставил; из governs genitive singular реестра. Из реестра names the source of the extract. |
| ru-05-0900 | C1i | reissue; Nominative singular feminine; повторная agrees with выдача, the subject of предусмотрена. Справки names the document whose reissue is in question. |
| ru-05-0901 | C1j | will issue; Third-person plural simple future; the human agent is unspecified. Perfective выдать; imperfective выдавать. Compare выдаст. It takes an accusative object; the future presents a completed issue. P: the unpacking preserves the unspecified issuer and tomorrow's timing. |
| ru-05-0902 | C1j | fell; Plural past reflexive form, agreeing with расходы. Perfective снизиться; imperfective снижаться. Compare снизятся. The changing quantity is the subject; the past presents the decrease as completed. P: the unpacking does not introduce an agent who reduced the expenses. |
| ru-05-0903 | C1j | cancelled; Plural past with an unspecified human subject. Perfective отменить; imperfective отменять. Compare отме́нят. It takes an accusative object; the past presents the cancellation as completed. P: both versions place the cancellation yesterday and leave the cancelling party unspecified. |
| ru-05-0904 | C1j | will check; Third-person plural simple future with an unspecified human subject. Perfective проверить; imperfective проверять. Compare проверит. It takes an accusative object; the future presents a completed check. P: the shorter wording preserves the source's unspecified checker. |
| ru-05-0905 | C1j | may be delayed; Third-person singular present может governs the perfective reflexive infinitive задержаться. Мочь is imperfective, with no simple aspect partner in this modal sense; compare могут. Задержаться is perfective; задерживаться is imperfective, and задержится is a future form. The flight is the subject; the construction expresses possibility. P: может retains the uncertainty in возможна. |
| ru-05-0906 | C1j | we ask; First-person plural present. Imperfective просить; perfective попросить. Compare про́сит. An infinitive states the requested action; the present makes the request. P: наша identifies the plural requester, and the verbal version retains the request. |
| ru-05-0907 | C1j | entry is possible; Perfective infinitive войти combines with the impersonal predicative можно. Войти is perfective; входить is imperfective. Compare войдёт. The prefix в- marks entry; the sentence does not specify the means of movement. The construction permits entry under the stated condition. The unprefixed imperfective base «идти» is unidirectional, contrasting with multidirectional «ходить»; prefixed «войти» is perfective here, paired aspectually with «входить». P: после оплаты remains the condition in both versions. |
| ru-05-0908 | C1j | was leaking out; Feminine singular past, agreeing with вода. Imperfective вытекать; perfective вытечь. Compare вытека́ет. Из governs the genitive source; вы- marks outward flow. The past describes the leak as a process. P: both versions describe yesterday's leak without asserting that all the water flowed out. |
| ru-05-0909 | C1j | return; Polite singular or plural imperative. Perfective вернуть; imperfective возвращать. Compare вернёт. It takes an accusative object; the imperative requests a completed return. P: the imperative expresses the request stated in the note and keeps its deadline. |
| ru-05-0910 | C1j | rose; Plural past, agreeing with цены. Perfective вырасти; imperfective расти. Compare вырастут. The quantity changing is the subject; the past reports a completed increase. P: the unpacking preserves the month and does not assign an agent. |
| ru-05-0911 | C1j | have not made; Negated plural past with an unspecified human subject. Perfective принять; imperfective принимать. Compare примут. It takes an accusative object; negation presents the decision as not yet made. P: пока retains the source's indication that the decision has not yet been made. |
| ru-05-0912 | C1j | will relocate; Third-person singular simple future, agreeing with отдел. Perfective переехать; imperfective переезжать. Compare перее́дут. Here пере- marks relocation to another place; the future presents the move as a completed event. The means and route are unspecified; directionality of the base motion verb does not specify them. P: both versions identify the department as the relocating unit and place the move in the following June. |
| ru-05-0913 | C1j | refused; Masculine singular past reflexive form. Feminine отказалась is also possible because поставщик may refer to a woman. Perfective отказаться; imperfective отказываться. Compare отка́жется. От governs the genitive of what is declined; the past reports a completed refusal. P: both versions identify the supplier as the refusing party and place the refusal yesterday. |
| ru-05-0914 | C1j | are waiting for; First-person plural present. Imperfective ждать; дождаться is a contextual perfective for waiting until the awaited event occurs. Compare ждут. First conjugation; compare объявля́ет. It takes an accusative object; the present announces the break immediately. Объявляю перерыв signals a pause in the meeting; на десять минут states its duration. |
| ru-05-1210 | C1m | take outside the scope of; Perfective infinitive with за and accusative plural рамки. Perfective вынести; imperfective выносить. Compare future вынесет. Вынести что за рамки чего takes an accusative issue and a dependent genitive; here it proposes excluding that issue from this meeting's scope. The phrase concerns the scope of discussion, not literal carrying. |
| ru-05-1211 | C1m | sign up; Imperfective reflexive infinitive after прошу. Imperfective записываться; perfective записаться. First conjugation; compare записывается. Записываться на что takes на with the accusative; the imperfective request describes the procedure for participants. Записываться на выступление means to put one's name down to speak. |
| ru-05-1212 | C1m | the order of turns; Accusative singular feminine inanimate; direct object of соблюдать. Соблюдать очерёдность asks participants to respect the stated sequence of speakers. |
| ru-05-1213 | C1m | a brief contribution; Nominative singular feminine; subject of будет. Реплика is a short contribution to the discussion. |
| ru-05-1214 | C1m | finish a thought; Perfective infinitive with accusative singular feminine мысль. Perfective закончить; imperfective заканчивать. Second conjugation; compare закончит. It takes an accusative object; the infinitive asks for the opportunity to complete the thought. Закончить мысль asks listeners to let the speaker complete what they are saying. |
| ru-05-1215 | C1m | state a view briefly; Perfective reflexive infinitive modified by an adverb. Perfective высказаться; imperfective высказываться. First conjugation; compare выскажется. It can take о with the prepositional for a topic; here the request concerns one brief contribution. Кратко limits the length of Oleg's requested contribution. |
| ru-05-1216 | C1m | bring the discussion back to the topic; First-person plural perfective future used with давайте; разговор is accusative and к теме takes the dative. Perfective вернуть; imperfective возвращать. First conjugation; compare вернёт. Вернуть разговор к чему takes an accusative discussion and к with the dative; the phrase proposes redirecting the discussion. This figurative phrase redirects a discussion to its stated subject. |
| ru-05-1217 | C1m | it has been decided; Short neuter singular passive participle used impersonally. Решить is perfective; imperfective решать. Second conjugation; compare реши́т. It can take a clause specifying the decision; the passive presents the following points as decided. Решено introduces the meeting outcome stated after the colon. |
| ru-05-1218 | C1m | have I understood correctly; Поняла is feminine singular past; понял is also possible because the speaker's gender is unspecified. Ли marks the question. Perfective понять; imperfective понимать. Compare future поймёт. The verb can take a clause expressing what was understood; the question checks the speaker's interpretation of the stated deadline. The speaker asks the group to confirm a specific understanding, without asserting agreement. |
| ru-05-1219 | C1m | we will record; First-person plural perfective future. Perfective отразить; imperfective отражать. Second conjugation; compare отразит. Отразить что в чём takes an accusative content and в with the prepositional; the future commits the group to recording it. Here отразить means to represent the views in the minutes. |
| ru-05-1220 | C1m | into the minutes; В governs accusative singular masculine протокол. Внести решение в протокол means to enter the decision in the meeting minutes. |
| ru-05-1221 | C1m | draft minutes; Проект is accusative singular masculine inanimate; протокола is dependent genitive singular. Проект протокола is a draft of the meeting record; the speaker invites comments on it. |
| ru-05-1222 | C1m | comments on the minutes; Замечания is accusative plural neuter inanimate; к governs dative singular протоколу. Замечания к протоколу are comments on the meeting record. |
| ru-05-1223 | C1m | an assigned task; Nominative singular neuter; the following infinitive specifies the assigned action. Анне identifies the person assigned the task. |
| ru-05-1224 | C1m | responsible; Instrumental singular feminine, referring to Ирину after назначить. Назначить кого ответственным за что assigns responsibility for the stated work. |
| ru-05-1225 | C1m | I will take care of; First-person singular perfective future reflexive. Perfective заняться; imperfective заниматься. Compare future займётся. Заняться чем governs the instrumental; the future commits the speaker to taking up the work. |
| ru-05-1226 | C1m | I will assign; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective закрепить; imperfective закреплять. Second conjugation; compare закрепит. Закрепить что за кем takes an accusative task and за with the instrumental; the future assigns it to the addressee. Закрепить задачу за человеком designates that person as responsible for it. |
| ru-05-1227 | C1m | I will tie; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective привязать; imperfective привязывать. Compare future привя́жет. Привязать что к чему takes an accusative item and к with the dative; the future commits the speaker to establishing the link. Привязать срок к дате makes the budget deadline dependent on the forecast's receipt date. |
| ru-05-1228 | C1m | promised to send; Feminine singular past обещала agrees with Анна; прислать is a perfective infinitive. Обещать is imperfective here; perfective пообещать. First conjugation; compare обещает. It takes an infinitive naming the commitment. Прислать is perfective; imperfective присылать. Compare future пришлёт. It takes an accusative item; the infinitive names the promised delivery. |
| ru-05-1229 | C1m | checkpoint date; Nominative singular feminine; контрольная agrees with дата. Контрольная дата identifies a scheduled checkpoint for the stated review. |
| ru-05-1230 | C1m | the deadline; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; крайний agrees with срок. Крайний срок names the last permissible time for the action specified by подачи правок. |
| ru-05-1231 | C1m | I will meet the deadline; First-person singular perfective future reflexive; в governs accusative срок. Perfective уложиться; imperfective укладываться. Second conjugation; compare уложится. Уложиться в срок uses в with the accusative; the commitment to finish on time is conditional. The figurative expression means to complete the work within the allotted time. |
| ru-05-1232 | C1m | I will notify; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective предупредить; imperfective предупреждать. Second conjugation; compare предупредит. It takes an accusative person and can specify the matter with о plus the prepositional or a clause; the future commits the speaker to giving notice if the delay occurs. |
| ru-05-1233 | C1m | dissenting opinion; Accusative singular neuter inanimate; особое agrees with мнение. Особое мнение is a dissenting position the speaker asks to put on record. |
| ru-05-1234 | C1m | against an early launch; Против governs genitive singular masculine запуска; досрочного agrees with it. Я против states the speaker's opposition; the following clause gives the reason. |
| ru-05-1235 | C1m | a counterproposal; Nominative singular neuter; встречное agrees with предложение. Встречное предложение introduces an alternative to the rollout approach under discussion. |
| ru-05-1236 | C1m | enter; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Perfective занести; imperfective заносить. Compare future занесёт. Занести что в протокол takes an accusative item and в with the accusative; the request seeks a completed entry in the minutes. The phrase concerns recording an objection, not literal carrying. |
| ru-05-1237 | C1m | in exchange for; The compound preposition governs accusative скидку. The phrase links the offered increase in order volume to the requested discount. |
| ru-05-1238 | C1m | you reduce; Second-person plural perfective future, used for polite address. Perfective сократить; imperfective сокращать. Second conjugation; compare сократит. It takes an accusative amount or payment; the future clause states a condition for accepting the schedule. |
| ru-05-1239 | C1m | payment in stages; Accusative singular feminine; поэтапную agrees with оплату. The phrase names the payment arrangement open to discussion; the speaker says they will not change the price. |
| ru-05-1240 | C1m | compare for agreement; Perfective infinitive after предлагаю. Сверить is perfective; imperfective сверять. Second conjugation; compare све́рит. Сверить что с чем takes an accusative item and с with the instrumental for the comparison; the infinitive proposes a single check. The speaker proposes comparing their understanding of the assignments with the colleagues' understanding. |
| ru-05-1241 | C1m | swap the order; Perfective infinitive with instrumental plural местами. Поменять is perfective; imperfective менять. Compare поменяет. Поменять что местами takes an accusative plural object; the infinitive proposes one change of order. The phrase proposes reversing the order of the presentations. |
| ru-05-1242 | C1m | section; Accusative singular masculine inanimate after в. Раздел «Разное» is the miscellaneous section of the meeting agenda. |
| ru-05-1243 | C1m | the most urgent; Genitive singular masculine superlative phrase, agreeing with вопроса after с. |
| ru-05-1244 | C1m | into one item; В governs accusative singular пункт; один agrees with it. Объединить что в один пункт proposes treating the two subjects as one agenda item. |
| ru-05-1245 | C1m | introduction; Accusative singular neuter inanimate, direct object of сокращу. Вступление is the opening part of the speaker's contribution. |
| ru-05-1246 | C1m | I will state; First-person singular perfective future. Обозначить is perfective; imperfective обозначать. Compare обозначит. It takes an accusative goal or issue; the future commits the speaker to stating it. |
| ru-05-1247 | C1m | materials for the meeting; Материалы is accusative plural inanimate; к governs dative singular совещанию. К совещанию identifies the meeting for which the materials are requested. |
| ru-05-1248 | C1m | presenter; Genitive singular masculine animate after нет. Докладчик is the person who presents the item to the meeting. |
| ru-05-1249 | C1m | move on to the next item; Perfective infinitive; к governs dative singular пункту. Перейти is perfective; imperfective переходить. Compare перейдёт. Here the prefixed verb describes a figurative transition to another agenda item; literal direction or means of travel is not at issue. «Перейти к чему» takes «к» with the dative. The proposal follows completion of the budget discussion. |
| ru-05-1250 | C1m | lead; Perfective infinitive after прошу. Подвести is perfective; imperfective подводить. Compare подведёт. Подвести кого к чему takes an accusative audience and к with the dative; here it requests a transition in the presentation. The phrase is figurative: the speaker asks Irina to lead the audience to the conclusion. |
| ru-05-1251 | C1m | raise a hand; Perfective infinitive with accusative singular feminine руку. Поднять is perfective; imperfective поднимать. Compare поднимет. Поднять руку takes an accusative object; the infinitive requests a signal from anyone wishing to ask a question. The raised hand signals a request for a turn. |
| ru-05-1252 | C1m | time for questions; Время is accusative singular neuter; для governs genitive plural вопросов. Для вопросов specifies the purpose of the time allocated after the presentation. |
| ru-05-1253 | C1m | may I ask a question; Impersonal можно introduces a request; вопрос is a singular masculine noun. This concise spoken request asks the presenter for a turn to ask about her calculation. |
| ru-05-1254 | C1m | a round of questions; Раунд is accusative singular masculine inanimate, the object of откроем; вопросов is dependent genitive plural. Раунд вопросов is a period set aside for questions after the presentation. |
| ru-05-1255 | C1m | both sides; Accusative feminine construction after выслушать; обе refers to two sides. Стороны refers to the participants holding different positions in the discussion. |
| ru-05-1256 | C1m | closing remarks; Accusative singular neuter after на; заключительное agrees with слово. Заключительное слово is a final contribution at the end of the discussion. |
| ru-05-1257 | C1m | I will summarize; First-person singular perfective future. Подытожить is perfective; imperfective подытоживать. Compare подытожит. It takes an accusative discussion or result; here the future introduces an immediate summary. |
| ru-05-1258 | C1m | we agreed on the price; По governs dative singular цене; договорились is plural past reflexive. Договориться is perfective; imperfective договариваться. Compare договорятся. Договориться по вопросу uses по with the dative to identify the matter; the past reports a completed agreement. The summary distinguishes the agreed price from the delivery date still to be discussed. |
| ru-05-1259 | C1m | outcome; Accusative singular masculine inanimate, direct object of уточню. Итог introduces the speaker's account of the assignments resulting from the discussion. |
| ru-05-1260 | C1m | provisional; Nominative singular neuter adjective used as a predicate, agreeing with решение. The decision is provisional; the following clause identifies a later budget approval. |
| ru-05-1261 | C1m | the matter settled; Вопрос is accusative singular masculine; instrumental закрытым refers to it after считать. Считать вопрос закрытым proposes treating the matter as settled in view of the stated agreement. |
| ru-05-1262 | C1m | has not been agreed; Negative short masculine singular passive participle, agreeing with срок. Согласовать is perfective; imperfective согласовывать. Compare согласует. Согласовать срок takes an accusative deadline; the passive describes its current unagreed status. |
| ru-05-1263 | C1m | I will make corrections; First-person singular perfective future with accusative plural исправления. Внести is perfective; imperfective вносить. Compare внесёт. Внести исправления в документ takes в with the accusative; the future commits the speaker to making the corrections. The expression concerns editing the minutes, not literal carrying. |
| ru-05-1264 | C1m | person assigned to carry out the task; Accusative singular masculine animate, direct object of укажу. Исполнитель identifies who is to carry out the task. |
| ru-05-1265 | C1m | allocate the tasks; Perfective infinitive with accusative plural inanimate задачи. Распределить is perfective; imperfective распределять. Compare распредели́т. The speaker proposes deciding which colleagues will carry out the tasks. |
| ru-05-1266 | C1m | will add to; Third-person singular perfective future, agreeing with Олег. Дополнить is perfective; imperfective дополнять. Compare дополню. Дополнить что чем takes an accusative item and instrumental material; the future assigns Oleg the addition. |
| ru-05-1267 | C1m | acceptance certificate; Акт is accusative singular masculine inanimate, the object of подпишу; приёмки is dependent genitive singular feminine. Акт приёмки is a document recording acceptance of completed work. |
| ru-05-1268 | C1m | I will take on the preparation; First-person singular perfective future with «на себя» and accusative singular feminine «подготовку». The speaker offers to take on preparation of the estimate if Oleg checks the calculations. |
| ru-05-1269 | C1m | the starting figures; Accusative plural inanimate; исходные agrees with цифры. Исходные цифры are the figures from which the calculation will be made. |
| ru-05-1270 | C1m | a consolidated version; Accusative singular feminine; единую agrees with версию. The version combines the colleagues' changes into one document. |
| ru-05-1271 | C1m | interim result; Accusative singular masculine inanimate; промежуточный agrees with результат. The interim result precedes the final result promised for Friday. |
| ru-05-1272 | C1m | the report submission deadline; Срок is nominative singular masculine; сдачи is dependent genitive singular feminine, and отчёта is genitive singular masculine dependent on сдачи. Срок сдачи отчёта names the deadline for submitting the report. |
| ru-05-1273 | C1m | working days; After два, дня has genitive singular form and рабочих has genitive plural form. Рабочие дни specify working days as the unit of time requested. |
| ru-05-1274 | C1m | with time to spare; С governs instrumental singular masculine запасом. Установить срок с запасом means to allow extra time within the deadline. |
| ru-05-1275 | C1m | that the mockup is ready; О governs prepositional singular готовности; макета is dependent genitive singular. Сообщить о готовности means to report that the specified item is ready. |
| ru-05-1276 | C1m | move the deadline; Perfective infinitive with accusative singular masculine срок. Перенести is perfective; imperfective переносить. Compare перенесёт. Перенести срок takes an accusative deadline; the infinitive requests one change. The phrase concerns rescheduling a deadline, not literal carrying. |
| ru-05-1277 | C1m | in the course of receipt; По governs dative singular мере; поступления is genitive singular and governs genitive данных. По мере поступления данных makes the ongoing checking dependent on the data arriving, with checking carried out as the data become available. |
| ru-05-1278 | C1m | divide the work into stages; Perfective infinitive; работу is accusative singular and на governs accusative plural этапы. Разбить is perfective; imperfective разбивать. Compare разобьёт. Разбить что на этапы takes an accusative task and на with the accusative; the infinitive proposes one division of the work. The phrase describes organizing work into stages. |
| ru-05-1279 | C1m | about the progress of the work; О governs prepositional singular ходе; работы is dependent genitive singular. Сообщить о ходе работы identifies the progress that the speaker will report. |
| ru-05-1280 | C1m | I vote against; First-person singular present; против indicates opposition in the vote. Голосовать is imperfective; perfective проголосовать. Compare голосу́ет. Голосовать против can take a genitive noun naming the proposal opposed; the present casts the speaker's vote now. The speaker casts a vote against the proposed deadline and gives a reason. |
| ru-05-1281 | C1m | order; Genitive singular masculine, dependent on согласования. Порядок обсуждения is the sequence in which the discussion will proceed. |
| ru-05-1282 | C1m | out of turn; Вне governs genitive singular очереди. The phrase proposes taking the payment issue before its usual turn in the meeting. |

| ru-05-1284 | C1m | continue; Imperfective infinitive after прошу. Imperfective продолжать; perfective продолжить. Compare продолжает. It can take an accusative activity or be used without an object; the request invites the speaker to continue her contribution. |
| ru-05-1285 | C1m | pause; Perfective infinitive after предлагаю. Perfective прервать; imperfective прерывать. Compare прервёт. It takes an accusative activity; the proposal calls for one temporary interruption. |
| ru-05-1286 | C1m | a clarification; Nominative singular neuter; короткое agrees with it. The speaker signals a brief clarification about the estimate before giving Oleg the floor. |

Следить is imperfective; no simple perfective partner expresses the same ongoing monitoring. Compare следи́т. Следить за чем takes the instrumental; the request applies throughout each contribution. Следить за временем asks speakers to monitor their elapsed time against the stated limit. |
| ru-05-1288 | C1m | for separate discussion; На governs accusative singular neuter обсуждение; отдельное agrees with it. Поставить вопрос на отдельное обсуждение proposes considering the purchasing issue separately. |
| ru-05-1289 | C1m | verbatim; Дословно specifies that the speaker will record the stated wording exactly. |
| ru-05-1290 | C1m | wording; Accusative singular feminine, direct object of уточню. Формулировка решения refers to how the decision is expressed. |
| ru-05-1291 | C1m | I will note; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective отметить; imperfective отмечать. Compare отметит. It can take a что-clause stating the point recorded; the future commits the speaker to noting it. |
| ru-05-1292 | C1m | further discussion; Nominative singular neuter; дополнительное agrees with обсуждение, the subject of требуется. The phrase identifies discussion still needed on the deadlines. |
| ru-05-1293 | C1m | I am handing over; First-person singular present. Imperfective передавать; perfective передать. Compare передаёт. Передавать что кому takes an accusative task and a dative recipient; the present makes the handover now. Передать проверку assigns the checking work to Irina. |
| ru-05-1294 | C1m | coordinator; Instrumental singular masculine occupational noun, referring to Анну after назначить. Назначить кого координатором assigns the named person the coordination role. |
| ru-05-1295 | C1m | I will check readiness; First-person singular perfective future with accusative singular feminine готовность. Perfective проверить; imperfective проверять. Compare проверит. Проверить готовность чего takes a genitive item; the future commits the speaker to one check. |
| ru-05-1296 | C1m | one day ahead of the deadline; На день states the amount of time; раньше takes genitive singular срока as the reference point. The phrase commits the speaker to completing the estimate one day before its deadline. |
| ru-05-1297 | C1m | a target; Instrumental singular masculine, referring to пятницу after считать. Считать дату ориентиром treats it as a working target rather than a confirmed date. |
| ru-05-1298 | C1m | I do not support; Negated first-person singular present. Imperfective поддерживать; perfective поддержать. Compare поддерживает. It takes an accusative proposal or plan; the present states the speaker's position now. |
| ru-05-1299 | C1m | we will agree to; First-person plural perfective future reflexive; на governs accusative предоплату. Perfective согласиться; imperfective соглашаться. Compare согласится. Согласиться на что takes the accusative; the future states conditional acceptance. |
| ru-05-1300 | C1m | my position; Accusative singular feminine; мою agrees with позицию, the object of указать. Позиция по графику is the speaker's stance on the schedule. |
| ru-05-1301 | C1n | I will devote; First-person singular perfective future. Perfective посвятить; imperfective посвящать. Compare посвятит. Посвятить что чему takes an accusative work and a dative topic; the future frames the presentation. |
| ru-05-1302 | C1n | my task; Nominative singular feminine; моя agrees with задача. The phrase frames the purpose of the speaker's presentation. |
| ru-05-1303 | C1n | I will focus; First-person singular perfective future reflexive. Perfective сосредоточиться; imperfective сосредоточиваться. Compare сосредоточится. Сосредоточиться на чём takes the prepositional; the future announces the presentation's focus. |
| ru-05-1304 | C1n | in closing; В governs accusative singular neuter заключение. The phrase signals the closing part of the planned presentation. |
| ru-05-1305 | C1n | I will dwell; First-person singular perfective future reflexive. Perfective остановиться; imperfective останавливаться. Compare остановится. In this figurative presentation frame остановиться на чём takes the prepositional; the future introduces a topic for fuller treatment. Остановиться на вопросе means to discuss it, not to stop physical movement. |
| ru-05-1306 | C1n | let us turn to; First-person plural perfective future reflexive; к governs dative тому. Обратиться is perfective; imperfective обращаться. Compare обрати́тся. Обратиться к чему takes the dative; the future marks an immediate transition in the talk. The speaker directs the audience's attention to the method used to measure waiting time. |
| ru-05-1307 | C1n | on the next slide; На governs prepositional singular masculine слайде; следующем agrees with it. The phrase directs the audience to the next visual in the presentation. |
| ru-05-1308 | C1n | represents; Third-person singular present, agreeing with линия. Imperfective обозначать; perfective обозначить. Compare обозначают. It takes an accusative quantity or concept; the present explains what the line represents. |
| ru-05-1309 | C1n | by accessibility; Под governs instrumental singular feminine доступностью. Понимать под чем что introduces the speaker's definition of a term. |
| ru-05-1310 | C1n | with an example; На governs prepositional singular masculine примере. Пояснить на примере introduces an example that illustrates the explanation. |
| ru-05-1311 | C1n | put simply; Проще is an invariable comparative; говоря is an imperfective adverbial participle. Говорить is imperfective; perfective сказать. Compare говорит. In this fixed parenthetical expression it introduces a simpler restatement. The expression signals that the speaker is explaining the term in simpler words. |
| ru-05-1312 | C1n | for clarity; Для governs genitive singular feminine наглядности. The phrase introduces a comparison intended to make the explanation easier to grasp. |
| ru-05-1313 | C1n | distinguish; Imperfective infinitive after важно. Imperfective различать; perfective различить. Compare различает. It takes accusative objects; here it names a distinction the audience should maintain. |
| ru-05-1314 | C1n | let me emphasize; First-person singular perfective future, introducing the statement after the colon. Perfective подчеркнуть; imperfective подчёркивать. Compare подчеркнёт. It can introduce a clause stating the point emphasized; the future performs the emphasis now. |
| ru-05-1315 | C1n | only weekdays; Genitive plural; будних agrees with дней after касается. Только restricts the calculation's stated scope to weekdays. |
| ru-05-1316 | C1n | in this comparison; В governs prepositional singular neuter сравнении; этом agrees with it. The phrase confines the stated treatment of weekends to the comparison being presented. |
| ru-05-1317 | C1n | as stated in; Указано is a short neuter passive participle used impersonally; в governs prepositional singular журнале. Указать is perfective; imperfective указывать. Compare ука́жет. It can introduce stated information; the passive attributes the count to the named source. The expression reports what the application log states without presenting an independent check. |
| ru-05-1318 | C1n | most often; The phrase identifies the most frequent request reported in the survey. |
| ru-05-1319 | C1n | it is unknown which; Неизвестно is an impersonal predicate; какие introduces an indirect question and agrees with plural курсы. The expression limits the survey result: interest in evening classes is known, but demand for particular courses is not. |

| ru-05-1321 | C1n | I will start with; First-person singular perfective future; с governs genitive обзора. Начать is perfective; imperfective начинать. Compare начнёт. Начать с чего names the first item; the future announces the opening of the talk. |

| ru-05-1323 | C1n | next; Далее signals the next step in the presentation. |

| ru-05-1325 | C1n | working definition; Nominative singular neuter; рабочее agrees with определение. The phrase introduces a definition adopted for the discussion. |
| ru-05-1326 | C1n | separately; The speaker signals that training costs will receive separate treatment. |
| ru-05-1327 | C1n | we will return to; First-person plural perfective future reflexive; к governs dative графику. Вернуться is perfective; imperfective возвращаться. Compare вернётся. The construction attributes the count to the log without claiming independent verification. |

| ru-05-1359 | C1n | I will be able to answer; First-person singular perfective future followed by a perfective infinitive. Смочь is perfective; imperfective мочь. Compare сможет. The future places the ability to answer after the report is checked. The speaker defers an answer about June expenses until after checking the report. |
| ru-05-1360 | C1n | for downloading; Для governs genitive singular neuter скачивания. Доступны для скачивания tells the audience that the materials can be downloaded. |
| ru-05-1361 | C1n | look at; Perfective infinitive after предлагаю; на governs accusative очередь. Compare target forms «взгляну» and «взглянет». The frame «взглянуть на что как на что» takes accusatives after «на». The speaker proposes treating the queue as part of the customer experience. |
